  if(typeof oldOpenComposer==='function'&&!oldOpenComposer.__evV20White){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldOpenComposer.apply(this,arguments);const input=$('evtcStageText');const args=arguments;const isFresh=input&&!String(input.value||'').trim()&&(!args[0]||args[0]==='__ethvelle_new_text_layer__');if(isFresh){const t=window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget?.();if(t)t.textColor='#FFFFFF';if(input){input.style.setProperty('color','#FFFFFF','important');input.style.setProperty('-webkit-text-fill-color','#FFFFFF','important');}}queueLayout();return result;};
    wrapped.__evV20White=true;window.openEthvelleTextComposer=wrapped;
  }
  // A compact stop-colour popover lives over the gradient editor and writes directly to the
  // stop's native input. The existing full colour wheel remains available from its Custom button.
  const nativeOpenStop=window.openEthvelleGradientStopColor;
  const stopColors=()=>[...new Set([...BASE,...readSaved()])];
  let stopPopover=null,stopButton=null,outsideHandler=null,escapeHandler=null;
  function closeStopPopover(){
    if(outsideHandler){document.removeEventListener('pointerdown',outsideHandler,true);outsideHandler=null;}
    if(escapeHandler){document.removeEventListener('keydown',escapeHandler,true);escapeHandler=null;}
    stopPopover?.remove();stopPopover=null;stopButton=null;
  }
  function dispatchStopColor(input,color){
    if(!input||!input.isConnected)return;input.value=color;input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
  }
  function openStopPalette(button){
    const input=button?.closest?.('.evce-stop')?.querySelector?.('[data-stop-color],input[type="color"]');if(!input)return;
    closeStopPopover();stopButton=button;
    const colors=stopColors(),split=Math.ceil(colors.length/2),rows=[colors.slice(0,split),colors.slice(split)];
    const pop=document.createElement('div');pop.id='evceStopColorPopover';pop.setAttribute('role','dialog');pop.setAttribute('aria-label','Gradient stop color');
    const head=document.createElement('div');head.className='evce-pop-head';const title=document.createElement('strong');title.className='evce-pop-title';title.textContent='Stop color';
    const actions=document.createElement('div');actions.style.cssText='display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;';
    const custom=document.createElement('button');custom.type='button';custom.className='evce-pop-custom';custom.setAttribute('aria-label','Custom color');custom.title='Custom color';custom.style.cssText='width:28px;height:28px;min-width:28px;border:0;border-radius:50%;background:conic-gradient(#ff1744,#ffb600,#d7ff00,#00d889,#00c9ff,#2f5ce8,#8955ff,#d946ef,#ff1744);';
    custom.onclick=e=>{e.preventDefault();const chosen=stopButton;closeStopPopover();if(typeof nativeOpenStop==='function')nativeOpenStop(chosen);};
    const close=document.createElement('button');close.type='button';close.className='evce-pop-close';close.setAttribute('aria-label','Close');close.textContent='×';close.style.cssText='width:30px;height:30px;min-width:30px;border:1px solid #44485d;border-radius:50%;background:#292c3d;color:#fff;font-size:23px;';close.onclick=closeStopPopover;
    actions.append(custom,close);head.append(title,actions);pop.appendChild(head);
    rows.forEach((rowColors,index)=>{const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='evce-pop-row';row.setAttribute('aria-label','Gradient colors row '+(index+1));rowColors.forEach(color=>{const sw=document.createElement('button');sw.type='button';sw.className='evce-pop-swatch';sw.style.backgroundColor=color;sw.title=color;sw.setAttribute('aria-label','Use '+color);sw.onclick=()=>{dispatchStopColor(input,color);closeStopPopover();};row.appendChild(sw);});pop.appendChild(row);});
    document.body.appendChild(pop);stopPopover=pop;
    const rect=button.getBoundingClientRect();const pr=pop.getBoundingClientRect();const left=Math.max(12,Math.min(window.innerWidth-pr.width-12,rect.left+rect.width/2-pr.width/2));
    let top=rect.top-pr.height-10;if(top<8)top=Math.min(window.innerHeight-pr.height-8,rect.bottom+8);top=Math.max(8,top);
    pop.style.left=Math.round(left)+'px';pop.style.top=Math.round(top)+'px';
    outsideHandler=e=>{if(!pop.contains(e.target)&&e.target!==button&&!button.contains(e.target))closeStopPopover();};
    escapeHandler=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')closeStopPopover();};
    setTimeout(()=>{if(stopPopover){document.addEventListener('pointerdown',outsideHandler,true);document.addEventListener('keydown',escapeHandler,true);}},0);
  }
  if(typeof nativeOpenStop==='function')window.openEthvelleGradientStopColor=openStopPalette;
  // Re-run the panel simplification on each open. Keep the real Apply action; never re-label
  // Save preset as Apply because that accidentally stores a preset on every tap.
  function simplifyGradientActions(){
    const editor=$('evpaCustomEffectEditor');if(!editor||!editor.querySelector('.evce-stops'))return;
    const actions=editor.querySelector('.evce-actions');if(!actions)return;
    const apply=actions.querySelector(':scope > [data-evce-action="apply"]');if(!apply)return;
    [...actions.children].forEach(b=>{if(b!==apply)b.remove();});
    apply.textContent='Apply';apply.classList.add('primary');apply.style.gridColumn='1';apply.style.display='flex';
    actions.style.gridTemplateColumns='minmax(0,1fr)';
  }
  const oldOpenGradient=window.openEthvelleCustomGradientEditor;
  if(typeof oldOpenGradient==='function'&&!oldOpenGradient.__evV20ApplyOnly){
    const wrapped=function(){const out=oldOpenGradient.apply(this,arguments);simplifyGradientActions();requestAnimationFrame(simplifyGradientActions);return out;};
    wrapped.__evV20ApplyOnly=true;window.openEthvelleCustomGradientEditor=wrapped;
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    const tab=e.target?.closest?.('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaTabs .evpa-tab');if(!tab)return;
    if(tab.dataset.tab!=='color')restoreColorTitleClones();
    queueLayout();
  },true);
  function boot(){install();queueLayout();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
  // No body-wide MutationObserver: it caused repeated palette rebuilds and visible flicker.

})();

(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const BASE=['#FFFFFF','#F5F5F7','#D9D9E0','#A3A3A3','#70717A','#414141','#17171C','#000000','#FFD9E2','#FFC5CF','#FF8C9A','#FA847C','#FF4E57','#FF174D','#D70D20','#8B0000','#FFE0B2','#FFB74D','#FF7B24','#FFD43B','#FFF176','#C6E64D','#B5E53B','#66BB6A','#25C98A','#16D9D0','#80DEEA','#13A7FF','#2F5CE8','#605DFF','#875BFA','#D946EF'];
  const norm=v=>/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(v||''))?String(v).toUpperCase():null;
  const dock=()=> $('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
  const palette=()=> $('evpaPalette');
  const currentTab=()=>dock()?.querySelector('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'';
  const currentTarget=()=>{try{return typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const keyForTab=()=>{const t=currentTab();return t==='stroke'?'strokeColor':t==='shadow'?'shadowColor':(t==='highlight'||t==='background')?'textBackgroundColor':'textColor';};
  const savedColors=()=>{try{const a=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ethvelleCustomTextColors')||'[]');return Array.isArray(a)?a.map(norm).filter(Boolean):[];}catch(_e){return [];}};
  const getSwatches=()=>palette()?.querySelector('.evpa-swatches')||null;
  const uniqueColors=nodes=>{const seen=new Set(),out=[];nodes.forEach(n=>{const c=norm(n?.dataset?.color);if(c&&!seen.has(c)){seen.add(c);out.push(c);}});return out;};
  function findSpecial(cls,swatches){return swatches?.querySelector('.'+cls)||dock()?.querySelector('.evpa-color-title-actions .'+cls)||dock()?.querySelector('#evpaPalette .'+cls)||null;}
  function makeSpecial(cls,label,title){const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className=cls;b.setAttribute('aria-label',label);b.title=title;return b;}
  function ensureSpecials(swatches){
    // v219-fix: prefer the real canvas-eyedropper button (ev-eyedrop-text) so a rebuilt row already contains it.
    let wheel=findSpecial('evpa-color-wheel',swatches),transparent=findSpecial('evpa-transparent',swatches),eye=findSpecial('ev-eyedrop-text',swatches)||findSpecial('evpa-eye',swatches);
    if(!wheel)wheel=makeSpecial('evpa-color-wheel','Custom color','Custom color');
    if(!transparent)transparent=makeSpecial('evpa-transparent','Transparent / none','Transparent / none');
    if(!eye){
      eye=makeSpecial('ev-eyedrop-button ev-eyedrop-text','Pick color from canvas','Pick color from canvas');
      eye.dataset.evEyedropButton='text';eye.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');
      eye.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m2 22 1-1h3l9-9"/><path d="M3 21v-3l9-9"/><path d="m15 6 3.4-3.4a2.1 2.1 0 1 1 3 3L18 9l.4.4a2.1 2.1 0 1 1-3 3l-3.8-3.8a2.1 2.1 0 1 1 3-3l.4.4Z"/></svg>';
    }
    // Remove any legacy plus/save button whenever this palette is rebuilt.
    swatches?.querySelectorAll('.evpa-save-swatch').forEach(b=>b.remove());
    dock()?.querySelectorAll('#evpaPalette .evpa-save-swatch').forEach(b=>b.remove());
    wheel.type='button';transparent.type='button';eye.type='button';
    wheel.setAttribute('aria-label','Custom color');wheel.title='Custom color';
    transparent.setAttribute('aria-label','Transparent');transparent.title='Transparent / none';
    if(!eye.classList.contains('ev-eyedrop-text')){
      eye.setAttribute('aria-label','Pick a color from the canvas');eye.title='Pick a color from the canvas';
      eye.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m2 22 1-1h3l9-9"/><path d="M3 21v-3l9-9"/><path d="m14 5 5 5"/><path d="m14 5 3-3a2.12 2.12 0 0 1 3 3l-3 3"/><path d="m9 9 6 6"/></svg>';
    }
    wheel.style.setProperty('background','conic-gradient(#ff1744,#ffb600,#d7ff00,#00d889,#00c9ff,#2f5ce8,#8955ff,#d946ef,#ff1744)','important');
    const group=dock()?.querySelector('.evpa-color-title-actions');
    if(group)group.remove();
    return {wheel,transparent,eye};
  }
  function makeColorSwatch(color,source){
    const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='evpa-swatch';b.dataset.color=color;
    b.style.backgroundColor=color;b.title=color;b.setAttribute('aria-label','Text colour '+color);
    if(source?.dataset?.customColor)b.dataset.customColor=source.dataset.customColor;
    return b;
  }
  function collectPaletteColors(swatches){
    // v219-fix: project custom colors (picked from the canvas) sit right beside Transparent, newest first;
    // the fixed base palette follows. A saved colour already in the base palette is not duplicated.
    const front=[...new Set(savedColors().filter(c=>!BASE.includes(c)))].reverse();
    return [...front,...BASE];
  }
  function syncMarkers(){
    const d=dock();if(!d)return;
    const t=currentTarget()||{};const key=keyForTab();
    const value=norm(t[key]||t.textColor||'#FFFFFF')||'#FFFFFF';
    const featureEnabled=key==='strokeColor'?(t.strokeEnabled===true||Number(t.strokeWidth)>0):key==='shadowColor'?!!t.shadowEnabled:key==='textBackgroundColor'?!!t.textBackgroundEnabled:true;
    const opacity=key==='textColor'?t.textOpacity:key==='strokeColor'?t.strokeOpacity:key==='shadowColor'?t.shadowOpacity:key==='textBackgroundColor'?t.textBackgroundOpacity:100;
    /* v225-fix: a chip the user just tapped stays marked even while Stroke/Shadow/Background is still switched off
       (before, it flashed blue for one frame and then lost its border). Nothing is marked by default while the effect is off. */
    const picked=(window.__ev224Picked||{})[String(t.id||'')+'|'+key];
    const showSel=!!featureEnabled||picked!==undefined;
    const transparentActive=showSel&&Number(opacity)===0&&(!!featureEnabled||picked==='__none__');
    /* v223-fix: exactly ONE selected chip - Transparent excludes every colour swatch and vice versa. */
    d.querySelectorAll('#evpaPalette .evpa-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',showSel&&!transparentActive&&norm(b.dataset.color)===value&&(!!featureEnabled||picked===value)));
    d.querySelectorAll('#evpaPalette .evpa-transparent').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',transparentActive));
  }
  window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches=syncMarkers;
  function fontLayout(){
    const d=dock(),swatches=getSwatches();if(!d||!swatches||currentTab()!=='font')return;
    const colors=collectPaletteColors(swatches),signature=colors.join('|');
    const existingTools=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-font-color-tools');
    const existingRow=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-font-color-row');
    if(existingTools&&existingRow&&swatches.classList.contains('evpa-v25-font-built')&&
       swatches.dataset.evpaV25FontColors===signature&&existingTools.querySelector('.evpa-color-wheel')&&
       existingTools.querySelector('.evpa-transparent')){syncMarkers();return;}
    const oldScrollLeft=swatches.scrollLeft;
    const special=ensureSpecials(swatches);
    const priorColorButtons=[...swatches.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]')];
    const sourceMap=new Map();priorColorButtons.forEach(b=>{const c=norm(b.dataset.color);if(c&&!sourceMap.has(c))sourceMap.set(c,b);});
    const maroonIndex=colors.indexOf('#8B0000');
    const split=maroonIndex>0?maroonIndex:Math.ceil(colors.length/2);
    const top=colors.slice(0,split),bottom=colors.slice(split);
    const tools=document.createElement('div');tools.className='evpa-font-color-tools';tools.setAttribute('aria-label','Custom, transparent and first row of text colours');
    const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='evpa-font-color-row';row.setAttribute('aria-label','Second row of text colours; swipe right-to-left to see more');
    if(special.eye&&special.eye.classList.contains('ev-eyedrop-text'))tools.appendChild(special.eye);
    tools.appendChild(special.wheel);tools.appendChild(special.transparent);
    top.forEach(c=>tools.appendChild(makeColorSwatch(c,sourceMap.get(c))));
    bottom.forEach(c=>row.appendChild(makeColorSwatch(c,sourceMap.get(c))));
    swatches.replaceChildren(tools,row);
    swatches.classList.add('evpa-v20-font-built','evpa-v25-font-built');
    swatches.dataset.evpaV25FontBuilt='1';
    swatches.dataset.evpaV25FontColors=signature;
    swatches.dataset.evpaV20Built=signature;
    const restore=()=>{if(swatches.isConnected)swatches.scrollLeft=Math.min(oldScrollLeft,Math.max(0,swatches.scrollWidth-swatches.clientWidth));};
    restore();requestAnimationFrame(restore);
    syncMarkers();
  }
  function colorLayout(){
    const d=dock(),swatches=getSwatches();if(!d||!swatches||currentTab()!=='color')return;
    const colors=collectPaletteColors(swatches),signature=colors.join('|');
    const existingGrid=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-color-grid.evpa-v25-color-grid');
    const existingWheel=existingGrid?.querySelector('.evpa-color-wheel');
    const existingTransparent=existingGrid?.querySelector('.evpa-transparent');
    if(existingGrid&&existingWheel&&existingTransparent&&existingGrid.dataset.evpaV25ColorColors===signature){
      swatches.classList.add('evpa-v25-color-built');syncMarkers();return;
    }
    const oldScrollTop=existingGrid?existingGrid.scrollTop:0;
    const special=ensureSpecials(swatches);
    const sourceMap=new Map();[...swatches.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]')].forEach(b=>{const c=norm(b.dataset.color);if(c&&!sourceMap.has(c))sourceMap.set(c,b);});
    const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='evpa-color-grid evpa-v25-color-grid';grid.setAttribute('aria-label','Color palette; swipe up and down for more colours');
    const realEye=!!(special.eye&&special.eye.classList.contains('ev-eyedrop-text'));
    if(realEye)grid.appendChild(special.eye);
    grid.appendChild(special.wheel);grid.appendChild(special.transparent);
    colors.forEach(c=>grid.appendChild(makeColorSwatch(c,sourceMap.get(c))));
    if(special.eye&&!realEye)grid.appendChild(special.eye);
    grid.dataset.evpaV25ColorBuilt='1';
    grid.dataset.evpaV25ColorColors=signature;
    swatches.replaceChildren(grid);
    swatches.classList.add('evpa-v25-color-built');
    const restore=()=>{if(grid.isConnected)grid.scrollTop=Math.min(oldScrollTop,Math.max(0,grid.scrollHeight-grid.clientHeight));};
    restore();requestAnimationFrame(restore);
    syncMarkers();
  }
  function syncModes(){
    const d=dock();if(!d)return;
    const tab=currentTab();
    d.classList.toggle('evpa-v25-font-mode',tab==='font');
    d.classList.toggle('evpa-v25-color-mode',tab==='color');
    if(tab==='font')fontLayout();else if(tab==='color')colorLayout();
    syncMarkers();
  }
  let layoutQueued=false;
  function queueLayout(){if(layoutQueued)return;layoutQueued=true;(window.requestAnimationFrame||((fn)=>setTimeout(fn,16)))(()=>{layoutQueued=false;try{syncModes();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle palette layout:',err);}});}
  window.addEventListener('ethvelle-project-color-scope',queueLayout);

  /* Two-way horizontal touch drag: both Font colour rows move together in either direction. */
  let drag=null,suppressClickUntil=0,suppressClickTarget=null;
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const scroller=e.target?.closest?.('#ethvellePicsartTextDock.evpa-v25-font-mode #evpaPalette .evpa-swatches.evpa-v25-font-built');
    if(!scroller||e.isPrimary===false||(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0))return;
    drag={id:e.pointerId,scroller,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,startScroll:scroller.scrollLeft,startButton:e.target?.closest?.('button')||null,moved:false};
  },{capture:true,passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
    if(!drag||drag.id!==e.pointerId)return;
    const dx=e.clientX-drag.startX,dy=e.clientY-drag.startY;
    if(!drag.moved&&Math.abs(dx)>7&&Math.abs(dx)>Math.abs(dy)*1.15)drag.moved=true;
    if(!drag.moved)return;
    const maxScroll=Math.max(0,drag.scroller.scrollWidth-drag.scroller.clientWidth);
    drag.scroller.scrollLeft=Math.max(0,Math.min(maxScroll,drag.startScroll-dx));
    suppressClickUntil=Date.now()+650;
    if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();
  },{capture:true,passive:false});
  const finishDrag=e=>{if(drag&&(!e||drag.id===e.pointerId)){if(drag.moved){suppressClickUntil=Date.now()+220;suppressClickTarget=drag.startButton;const oldTarget=suppressClickTarget;setTimeout(()=>{if(suppressClickTarget===oldTarget&&Date.now()>=suppressClickUntil)suppressClickTarget=null;},240);}drag=null;}};
  window.addEventListener('pointerup',finishDrag,{capture:true,passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('pointercancel',finishDrag,{capture:true,passive:true});

  /* Delegate palette taps before legacy inline handlers: every swatch uses the same live target API,
     so first-time text, existing text, duplicate Font swatches and the Color grid stay in sync. */
  window.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    const d=dock();if(!d||!e.target?.closest)return;
    const swatch=e.target.closest('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-swatch[data-color],#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-color-wheel,#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-transparent,#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-eye');
    if(!swatch)return;
    if(Date.now()<suppressClickUntil&&suppressClickTarget&&(e.target===suppressClickTarget||suppressClickTarget.contains(e.target))){suppressClickTarget=null;e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();return;}
    if(Date.now()>=suppressClickUntil)suppressClickTarget=null;
    const key=keyForTab();
    e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    try{if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColorTarget==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetColorTarget(key);}catch(_e){}
    if(swatch.classList.contains('evpa-swatch')&&swatch.hasAttribute('data-color')){
      const color=norm(swatch.dataset.color);if(!color)return;
      try{if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColor==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetColor(key,color);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle swatch selection:',err);}
      syncMarkers();return;
    }
    if(swatch.classList.contains('evpa-color-wheel')){
      try{if(typeof window.openEthvelleColorPicker==='function')window.openEthvelleColorPicker(key);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle custom color picker:',err);}
      return;
    }
    if(swatch.classList.contains('evpa-transparent')){
      try{if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetNone==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetNone(key);else if(key==='textColor'&&typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetValue==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('textOpacity',0);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle transparent swatch:',err);}
      syncMarkers();return;
    }
    if(swatch.classList.contains('evpa-eye')){
      try{if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSampleColor==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSampleColor();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle canvas eyedropper:',err);}
    }
  },true);

  const originalSetColor=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
  if(typeof originalSetColor==='function'&&!originalSetColor.__evV25PaletteSync){
    const wrappedSetColor=function(){const out=originalSetColor.apply(this,arguments);queueLayout();return out;};
    wrappedSetColor.__evV25PaletteSync=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=wrappedSetColor;
  }
  const originalSetTool=window.ethvellePicsartSetTool;
  if(typeof originalSetTool==='function'&&!originalSetTool.__evV25PaletteSync){
    const wrappedSetTool=function(){const out=originalSetTool.apply(this,arguments);queueLayout();return out;};
    wrappedSetTool.__evV25PaletteSync=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetTool=wrappedSetTool;
  }
  const originalOpenComposer=window.openEthvelleTextComposer;
  if(typeof originalOpenComposer==='function'&&!originalOpenComposer.__evV25PaletteSync){
    const wrappedOpenComposer=function(){const out=originalOpenComposer.apply(this,arguments);queueLayout();return out;};
    wrappedOpenComposer.__evV25PaletteSync=true;window.openEthvelleTextComposer=wrappedOpenComposer;
  }
  let observed=null,observer=null;
  function attach(){const p=palette();if(!p||p===observed)return;if(observer)observer.disconnect();observed=p;observer=new MutationObserver(()=>queueLayout());observer.observe(p,{childList:true,subtree:true});queueLayout();}
  function boot(){attach();syncModes();syncMarkers();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
  const bodyObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>{if(dock()&&palette()!==observed)attach();});
  if(document.body)bodyObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  window.addEventListener('resize',queueLayout,{passive:true});
})();
</script>


<style id="ethvelle-capcut-timeline-layout-v4">
  /* Compact CapCut-like tracks give the portrait preview more vertical room while keeping
     four default rows visible without a vertical swipe on typical phone screens. */
  .timeline-area{height:clamp(204px,25dvh,236px)!important;min-height:204px!important;max-height:236px!important;flex:0 0 auto!important;}
  .timeline-scroll{overflow-x:auto!important;overflow-y:auto!important;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch!important;overscroll-behavior:contain;scroll-behavior:auto!important;}
  .timeline-inner{padding-top:4px!important;padding-bottom:4px!important;}
  .timeline-ruler{height:14px!important;margin-bottom:3px!important;}
  #timelineInner .layer-row[data-layer="background"]{height:42px!important;min-height:42px!important;max-height:42px!important;}
  #timelineInner #track-background{height:36px!important;min-height:36px!important;max-height:36px!important;}
  #timelineInner .track-clip{top:0!important;height:36px!important;min-height:36px!important;max-height:36px!important;}
  #timelineInner .layer-row.char-row{height:39px!important;min-height:39px!important;max-height:39px!important;align-items:flex-start!important;}
  #timelineInner .layer-row.char-row .layer-badge{margin-top:6px!important;}
  #timelineInner .char-row-track{height:35px!important;min-height:35px!important;max-height:35px!important;margin-top:1px!important;margin-bottom:1px!important;}
  #timelineInner .anim-clip{height:31px!important;}
  #timelineInner .layer-row.overlay-track-row{min-height:38px!important;align-items:flex-start!important;padding-top:0!important;padding-bottom:0!important;}
  #timelineInner .layer-row.overlay-track-row .layer-badge{margin-top:6px!important;}
  #timelineInner .layer-row.overlay-track-row .layer-track{min-height:34px!important;margin-top:2px!important;margin-bottom:2px!important;}
  #timelineInner .overlay-clip{height:30px!important;}
  #timelineInner .layer-row.char-add-row{height:34px!important;min-height:34px!important;max-height:34px!important;}
  #timelineInner .char-add-row .layer-badge{width:20px!important;height:20px!important;margin-top:5px!important;}
  #timelineInner .char-add-row .char-add-track{height:28px!important;min-height:28px!important;max-height:28px!important;font-size:11px!important;}
  /* Portrait projects use nearly the entire available preview box, like CapCut. */
  .canvas-wrap.canvas-wrap-portrait{padding:2px 4px!important;}
</style>

<style id="ethvelle-v30-compact-selection-animation-filter">
  /* Keep selected clip edges INSIDE their compact timeline row. Previous styling intentionally
     projected 20px handles 10px beyond selected clips; once compact rows were introduced those
     handles painted into adjacent tracks and made the selected clip appear much taller. */
  
  #timelineInner .anim-clip.selected,
  #timelineInner .overlay-clip.selected,
  #timelineInner .track-clip.selected{
    z-index:6!important;box-sizing:border-box!important;
  }
  #timelineInner .anim-clip.selected{height:31px!important;min-height:31px!important;max-height:31px!important;}
  #timelineInner .overlay-clip.selected{height:30px!important;min-height:30px!important;max-height:30px!important;}
/* The Add-character lane used a 20px badge while every other track uses 26px; that made its
     dashed placeholder begin 6px too far left. Match the normal badge and retain the same gap. */
  #timelineInner .layer-row.char-add-row{display:flex!important;align-items:center!important;gap:10px!important;}
  #timelineInner .char-add-row .char-add-track{flex:1 1 auto!important;min-width:0!important;height:28px!important;min-height:28px!important;max-height:28px!important;margin:0!important;box-sizing:border-box!important;}
  /* New clip tools keep the existing horizontal toolbar layout and touch target sizing. */
  #bottomToolsClip .evca-toolbar-tool{display:flex;flex:0 0 78px;min-width:78px;max-width:82px;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:5px;padding:3px 4px;}
  #bottomToolsClip .evca-toolbar-tool svg{width:21px;height:21px;flex:0 0 21px;}
  #bottomToolsClip .evca-toolbar-tool span{font-size:10px;line-height:1.12;white-space:normal;text-align:center;max-width:100%;}
  .evca-panel{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:14px;padding-bottom:8px;}
  .evca-panel-note{font-size:11px;line-height:1.45;color:var(--text-dim);margin:0;}
  .evca-tabs{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));gap:7px;}
  .evca-tab{min-width:0;padding:10px 5px;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--panel);color:var(--text-dim);font-size:11px;font-weight:700;line-height:1.2;}
  .evca-tab.active{color:#fff;background:rgba(47,92,232,.2);border-color:var(--accent);box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(47,92,232,.15);}
  .evca-section-title{font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);margin:0 0 8px;}
  .evca-preset-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));gap:8px;}
  .evca-preset{min-width:0;min-height:64px;padding:9px 4px;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--panel);color:var(--text-dim);display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:6px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;line-height:1.15;}
  .evca-preset:before{content:'';width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:8px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#697bff,#212b57);box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,.12);}
  .evca-preset[data-preset="fade"]:before{background:linear-gradient(135deg,transparent,#aabaff);}
  .evca-preset[data-preset^="slide"]:before{background:linear-gradient(90deg,#252b4d,#8197ff);}
  .evca-preset[data-preset^="zoom"]:before{background:radial-gradient(circle,#dce4ff 0 20%,#697bff 23% 55%,#252b4d 58%);}
  .evca-preset[data-preset="none"]:before{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#262630,#424250);}
  .evca-preset.active{border-color:var(--accent);color:#fff;background:rgba(47,92,232,.15);box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(47,92,232,.14);}
  .evca-duration{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;background:var(--panel);padding:12px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;}
  .evca-duration-top,.evca-filter-head{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text);}
  .evca-duration output,.evca-filter-head output{color:#9db4ff;font-size:12px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;}
  .evca-panel input[type="range"]{display:block;width:100%;margin:0;accent-color:var(--accent);touch-action:pan-y;}
  .evca-filter-presets{display:flex;gap:8px;overflow-x:auto;padding:3px 2px 7px;scrollbar-width:none;}
  .evca-filter-presets::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none;}
  .evca-filter-preset{flex:0 0 68px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:6px;color:var(--text-dim);font-size:10px;font-weight:700;}
  .evca-filter-swatch{width:54px;height:46px;display:block;border:2px solid transparent;border-radius:9px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#7d8ba7,#e3b4a2 48%,#213454);}
  .evca-filter-preset.active .evca-filter-swatch{border-color:#fff;box-shadow:0 0 0 2px var(--accent);}
  .evca-filter-swatch.warm{filter:sepia(.34) saturate(1.24);}.evca-filter-swatch.cool{filter:hue-rotate(24deg) saturate(1.1);}
  .evca-filter-swatch.vivid{filter:saturate(1.65) contrast(1.12);}.evca-filter-swatch.bw{filter:grayscale(1);}
  .evca-filter-swatch.sepia{filter:sepia(.75);}.evca-filter-swatch.dramatic{filter:contrast(1.4) saturate(.8);}
  .evca-filter-field{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:7px;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.06);}
  .evca-filter-reset{min-height:42px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;background:var(--elevated);color:var(--text);font-size:12px;font-weight:700;}
  @media(max-width:360px){.evca-preset-grid{gap:6px;}.evca-preset{min-height:58px;}.evca-tabs{gap:5px;}.evca-tab{font-size:10px;}}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v30-clip-animations-and-filters">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const defaults = {brightness:100,contrast:100,saturation:100,hue:0,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:0};
  let animCategory = 'in';
  let panelPersistTimer = 0;
  let previewRenderRaf = 0;
  const visualTypes = new Set(['image','video','text']);
  const clamp = (v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):min));
  const getOverlays = ()=>typeof overlays!=='undefined' && Array.isArray(overlays) ? overlays : [];
  const getBackgrounds = ()=>typeof backgrounds!=='undefined' && Array.isArray(backgrounds) ? backgrounds : [];
  const selectedId = ()=>String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined' || selectedClip==null ? '' : selectedClip);
  const isCharClipId=id=>/^(main|seg\d+)$/.test(String(id||''));
  function selectedCharClip(){const id=selectedId();if(!isCharClipId(id)||typeof getClipByRef!=='function')return null;try{return getClipByRef(id)||null;}catch(_e){return null;}}
  // v299: a selected character animation clip is a valid Clip-animation target as well.
  function isVisualModel(o){return !!o&&(visualTypes.has(o.type)||o===selectedCharClip());}
  function overlayForSelected(){return getOverlays().find(o=>String(o.id)===selectedId())||selectedCharClip()||null;}
  function backgroundForSelected(){return getBackgrounds().find(b=>String(b.id)===selectedId())||null;}
  function currentVisualTarget(){
    const ov=overlayForSelected();
    if(ov && visualTypes.has(ov.type)) return {kind:'overlay',model:ov};
    const bg=backgroundForSelected();
    if(bg) return {kind:'background',model:bg};
    return null;
  }
  function getAnimationConfig(ov){
    if(!ov.clipAnimations || typeof ov.clipAnimations!=='object') ov.clipAnimations={};
    const c=ov.clipAnimations;
    c.in = c.in || 'none'; c.out = c.out || 'none'; c.overall = c.overall || 'none';
    c.inDuration = clamp(c.inDuration==null?0.45:c.inDuration,0.1,2.5);
    c.outDuration = clamp(c.outDuration==null?0.45:c.outDuration,0.1,2.5);
    c.overallDuration = clamp(c.overallDuration==null?1.2:c.overallDuration,0.25,4);
    return c;
  }
  const eased = p=>{p=clamp(p,0,1);return p*p*(3-2*p);};
  /* v362: 16 extra In/Out presets. Each one only uses the five channels every renderer already
     understands (opacity, scale, x, y, rotation), so canvas, timeline preview and export all match. */
  const _eo3=u=>1-Math.pow(1-u,3), _ei3=u=>u*u*u;
  const _eoBack=(u,k)=>{k=k==null?1.70158:k;const c=u-1;return 1+(k+1)*c*c*c+k*c*c;};
  const _eoElastic=u=>u<=0?0:u>=1?1:Math.pow(2,-9*u)*Math.sin((u*10-0.75)*(2*Math.PI/3))+1;
  const _eoBounce=u=>{const n=7.5625,d=2.75;if(u<1/d)return n*u*u;if(u<2/d){u-=1.5/d;return n*u*u+0.75;}if(u<2.5/d){u-=2.25/d;return n*u*u+0.9375;}u-=2.625/d;return n*u*u+0.984375;};
  const _S=(o,s,x,y,r)=>({opacity:o,scale:s,x:x,y:y,rotation:r});
  const _mirror=fn=>(u,c)=>fn(1-u,c);
  const EXT_PRESETS={
    'bounce':   {in:(u,c)=>_S(Math.min(1,u*5),1,0,-c.by*(1-_eoBounce(u)),0)},
    'elastic':  {in:(u)=>_S(Math.min(1,u*6),Math.max(0.05,_eoElastic(u)),0,0,0)},
    'spin':     {in:(u)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(Math.min(1,u*4),0.15+0.85*e,0,0,-360*(1-e));}},
    'swirl':    {in:(u)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(Math.min(1,u*3),0.05+0.95*e,0,0,-540*(1-e));}},
    'swing':    {in:(u,c)=>_S(Math.min(1,u*5),1,0,-c.dy*0.3*(1-_eo3(u)),38*Math.pow(1-u,1.3)*Math.cos(u*Math.PI*3.5))},
    'wobble':   {in:(u,c)=>_S(Math.min(1,u*5),1,-c.dx*0.8*(1-_eo3(u)),0,14*(1-u)*Math.sin(u*Math.PI*5))},
    'drop':     {in:(u,c)=>_S(Math.min(1,u*4),1,0,-c.by*(1-_eoBack(u,1.1)),0)},
    'rise':     {in:(u,c)=>_S(Math.min(1,u*4),1,0,c.by*(1-_eoBack(u,1.1)),0)},
    'fade-left':{in:(u,c)=>_S(u,1,-c.dx*0.7*(1-_eo3(u)),0,0)},
    'fade-right':{in:(u,c)=>_S(u,1,c.dx*0.7*(1-_eo3(u)),0,0)},
    'fade-up':  {in:(u,c)=>_S(u,1,0,c.dy*0.7*(1-_eo3(u)),0)},
    'fade-down':{in:(u,c)=>_S(u,1,0,-c.dy*0.7*(1-_eo3(u)),0)},
    'whip':     {in:(u,c)=>_S(Math.min(1,u*6),1,c.bx*(1-_eoBack(u,2.4)),0,7*(1-u))},
    'grow':     {in:(u)=>_S(Math.min(1,u*3),0.05+0.95*(1-Math.pow(1-u,2)),0,0,0)},
    'glitch':   {in:(u,c)=>{const q=1-u,j=Math.sin(u*47)*Math.sin(u*131);
                   return _S(Math.min(1,u*3)*(u>=0.85?1:(Math.sin(u*83+1)>-0.25?1:0.15)),1+0.05*q*Math.sin(u*61),j*c.dx*0.35*q,Math.sin(u*97)*c.dy*0.08*q,j*3*q);}},
    'flicker':  {in:(u)=>_S(u>=0.9?1:clamp(u*1.1+0.45*Math.sin(u*70),0,1),1,0,0,0)},
    /* v364: 16 more In/Out presets (same five channels: opacity, scale, x, y, rotation). */
    'zoom-fade':  {in:(u)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(e,1.35-0.35*e,0,0,0);}},
    'tilt-left':  {in:(u,c)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(Math.min(1,u*3),1,-c.dx*0.9*(1-e),0,-28*(1-e));}},
    'tilt-right': {in:(u,c)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(Math.min(1,u*3),1,c.dx*0.9*(1-e),0,28*(1-e));}},
    'roll-left':  {in:(u,c)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(Math.min(1,u*4),1,-c.bx*(1-e),0,-360*(1-e));}},
    'roll-right': {in:(u,c)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(Math.min(1,u*4),1,c.bx*(1-e),0,360*(1-e));}},
    'tada':       {in:(u)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(Math.min(1,u*6),0.35+0.65*e+0.12*Math.sin(u*Math.PI)*u,0,0,7*(1-u)*Math.sin(u*Math.PI*7));}},
    'rubber':     {in:(u)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(Math.min(1,u*6),0.2+0.8*e+0.22*(1-u)*Math.sin(u*Math.PI*6),0,0,0);}},
    'zoom-spin':  {in:(u)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(e,2.4-1.4*e,0,0,200*(1-e));}},
    'slam':       {in:(u,c)=>_S(Math.min(1,u*8),1+1.7*Math.pow(1-u,3),Math.sin(u*80)*c.dx*0.05*Math.pow(1-u,2),0,0)},
    'zoom-bounce':{in:(u)=>_S(Math.min(1,u*5),Math.max(0.05,_eoBack(u,2.6)),0,0,0)},
    'tumble':     {in:(u,c)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(Math.min(1,u*5),1,0,-c.by*(1-_eoBounce(u)),270*(1-e));}},
    'zigzag':     {in:(u,c)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(Math.min(1,u*4),1,c.dx*0.9*(1-u)*Math.sin(u*Math.PI*6),-c.by*(1-e),0);}},
    'launch':     {in:(u,c)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(Math.min(1,u*5),0.75+0.25*e,0,c.by*2.2*(1-_eoBack(u,1.2)),8*(1-e));}},
    'shiver':     {in:(u,c)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(Math.min(1,u*4),0.9+0.1*e,Math.sin(u*Math.PI*14)*c.dx*0.22*Math.pow(1-u,2),0,Math.sin(u*Math.PI*11)*4*(1-u));}},
    'diagonal':   {in:(u,c)=>{const e=_eo3(u);return _S(Math.min(1,u*3),1,-c.dx*1.1*(1-e),-c.dy*1.1*(1-e),0);}},
    'orbit':      {in:(u,c)=>{const e=_eo3(u),a=(1-e)*Math.PI*2.2,r=1-e;return _S(Math.min(1,u*4),0.4+0.6*e,c.dx*1.4*r*Math.cos(a),c.dy*1.4*r*Math.sin(a),0);}}
  };
  function extPhaseState(name,phase,progress,w,h){
    const def=EXT_PRESETS[name];if(!def)return null;
    const u=clamp(progress,0,1);
    const ctx={dx:Math.max(36,w*0.18),dy:Math.max(36,h*0.18),bx:Math.max(80,w*0.45),by:Math.max(80,h*0.3)};
    const fn=phase==='in'?def.in:(def.out||_mirror(def.in));
    return fn(u,ctx);
  }
  function phaseState(name,phase,progress,w,h){
    const p=eased(progress), out={opacity:1,scale:1,x:0,y:0,rotation:0};
    if(!name||name==='none') return out;
    const extState=extPhaseState(name,phase,progress,w,h);if(extState)return extState;
    // v363: Out is the exact reverse of In (q runs 0->1 for In, 1->0 for Out).
    const q=phase==='in'?p:1-p;
    if(name==='fade'){out.opacity=q;return out;}
    if(name==='zoom-in'){out.scale=0.58+0.42*q;return out;}
    if(name==='zoom-out'){out.scale=1.32-0.32*q;return out;}
    if(name==='pop'){out.scale=0.22+0.78*q;out.opacity=0.45+0.55*q;return out;}
    if(name==='rotate'){out.rotation=-150*(1-q);out.scale=0.86+0.14*q;return out;}
    const dx=Math.max(36,w*0.18),dy=Math.max(36,h*0.18);
    if(name==='slide-left') out.x=phase==='in'?-dx*(1-p):-dx*p;
    else if(name==='slide-right') out.x=phase==='in'?dx*(1-p):dx*p;
    else if(name==='slide-up') out.y=dy*(1-q);
    else if(name==='slide-down') out.y=-dy*(1-q);
    return out;
  }
  /* v411: every Overall loop lives in ONE function so the clip on the canvas, the export and the
     little preview tiles in the panel always play exactly the same motion. */
  function applyOverallMotion(result,overall,rel,period,phase,w,h){
    const f=(Math.max(0,rel)%period)/period;
    if(overall==='pulse') result.scale*=1+0.055*(0.5+0.5*Math.sin(phase));
    else if(overall==='zoom') result.scale*=1+0.075*(0.5+0.5*Math.sin(phase));
    else if(overall==='float') result.y+=Math.sin(phase)*Math.min(12,h*0.025);
    else if(overall==='shake') result.x+=Math.sin(phase*3)*Math.min(5,w*0.012);
    else if(overall==='rotate') result.rotation+=Math.sin(phase)*4;
    else if(overall==='heartbeat'){const beat=Math.exp(-Math.pow((f-0.12)/0.06,2))+0.75*Math.exp(-Math.pow((f-0.3)/0.07,2));result.scale*=1+0.09*beat;}
    else if(overall==='sway'){result.rotation+=Math.sin(phase)*6;result.x+=Math.sin(phase)*Math.min(8,w*0.02);}
    else if(overall==='jelly'){result.scale*=1+0.07*Math.sin(phase*2);result.rotation+=Math.sin(phase*2+1)*2.2;}
    else if(overall==='spin-loop'){result.rotation+=f*360;}
    else if(overall==='wiggle'){result.rotation+=Math.sin(phase*4)*3.5;result.x+=Math.sin(phase*4+0.5)*Math.min(3,w*0.008);}
    else if(overall==='bob'){result.y-=Math.abs(Math.sin(phase/2))*Math.min(14,h*0.03);}
    else if(overall==='breathe'){result.scale*=1+0.035*(0.5-0.5*Math.cos(phase));result.y+=Math.sin(phase)*Math.min(4,h*0.008);}
    else if(overall==='flash'){result.opacity*=0.35+0.65*(0.5+0.5*Math.cos(phase*2));}
    else if(overall==='pendulum'){result.rotation+=Math.sin(phase)*11;result.y+=(1-Math.cos(phase))*Math.min(4,h*0.008);}
    else if(overall==='drift'){result.x+=Math.sin(phase)*Math.min(14,w*0.035);result.y+=Math.sin(phase*2)*Math.min(7,h*0.014);}
    else if(overall==='tremble'){result.x+=Math.sin(phase*9)*Math.min(2.4,w*0.006);result.y+=Math.cos(phase*11)*Math.min(2.4,h*0.004);}
    else if(overall==='hop'){const u=Math.abs(Math.sin(f*Math.PI));result.y-=u*Math.min(22,h*0.04);result.scale*=1+0.04*(1-u);}
    else if(overall==='glow'){result.opacity*=0.62+0.38*(0.5+0.5*Math.sin(phase));}
    else if(overall==='tilt'){result.rotation+=Math.sin(phase)*3;result.scale*=1+0.03*(0.5+0.5*Math.sin(phase));}
    else if(overall==='swell'){result.scale*=1+0.12*(0.5-0.5*Math.cos(phase));}
  }
  window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState = function(ov,tSec){
    const neutral={opacity:1,scale:1,x:0,y:0,rotation:0};
    if(!ov || !ov.clipAnimations || typeof ov.clipAnimations!=='object') return neutral;
    const c=ov.clipAnimations;
    const start=Number(ov.trimStart)||0;
    const end=Number.isFinite(Number(ov.trimEnd))?Number(ov.trimEnd):start;
    const t=Number.isFinite(Number(tSec))?Number(tSec):start;
    const len=Math.max(0.05,end-start);
    const w=Math.max(120,Number(typeof stageW!=='undefined'?stageW:0)||360);
    const h=Math.max(120,Number(typeof stageH!=='undefined'?stageH:0)||640);
    const result={...neutral};
    const add=(v)=>{result.opacity*=v.opacity;result.scale*=v.scale;result.x+=v.x;result.y+=v.y;result.rotation+=v.rotation;};
    const inName=c.in||'none',outName=c.out||'none';
    const inDur=Math.min(len,clamp(c.inDuration==null?0.45:c.inDuration,0.1,2.5));
    const outDur=Math.min(len,clamp(c.outDuration==null?0.45:c.outDuration,0.1,2.5));
    if(inName!=='none' && t<start+inDur) add(phaseState(inName,'in',(t-start)/Math.max(0.001,inDur),w,h));
    if(outName!=='none' && t>=end-outDur) add(phaseState(outName,'out',(t-(end-outDur))/Math.max(0.001,outDur),w,h));
    const overall=c.overall||'none';
    if(overall!=='none' && t>=start && t<end){
      const period=clamp(c.overallDuration==null?1.2:c.overallDuration,0.25,4);
      const phase=((Math.max(0,t-start)%period)/period)*Math.PI*2;
      applyOverallMotion(result,overall,Math.max(0,t-start),period,phase,w,h);
    }
    result.opacity=clamp(result.opacity,0,1);result.scale=clamp(result.scale,0.05,3);
    return result;
  };
  function schedulePreview(){
    if(previewRenderRaf) return;
    previewRenderRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      previewRenderRaf=0;
      try{if(typeof renderTimelineScrubPreview==='function')renderTimelineScrubPreview();else if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle preview update:',e);}
    });
  }
  let clipAnimationPreviewRaf = 0;
  let clipAnimationPreviewToken = 0;
  function renderClipAnimationPreviewFrame(){
    try{if(typeof renderTimelineScrubPreview==='function')renderTimelineScrubPreview();else if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}
    catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle clip preview frame:',e);}
  }
  function previewClipAnimationWindow(ov, category){
    // This preview belongs to an image/video/text clip, not to the character rig. A
    // media-only project therefore uses the same clock and compositor without a character.
    if(!ov || !isVisualModel(ov)) return;
    const c=getAnimationConfig(ov);
    const start=Math.max(0,Number(ov.trimStart)||0);
    const end=Number.isFinite(Number(ov.trimEnd))?Number(ov.trimEnd):start;
    const len=Math.max(0,end-start);
    if(len<0.05) return;

    let from=start, to=end;
    if(category==='in'){
      // In: preview exactly from the clip's first frame for the chosen In duration.
      const span=Math.min(len,clamp(c.inDuration==null?0.45:c.inDuration,0.1,2.5));
      from=start;
      to=start+span;
    }else if(category==='out'){
      // Out: preview the final part of the clip. Example: a 5s clip / 1s Out = 4s to 5s.
      // v362: play the TAIL of the clip once: ~1s of normal playback, then the Out animation to the last frame.
      const span=Math.min(len,clamp(c.outDuration==null?0.45:c.outDuration,0.1,2.5));
      const lead=Math.min(1.0,Math.max(0,len-span));
      from=end-span-lead;
      to=end;
    }else{
      const cycle=clamp(c.overallDuration==null?1.2:c.overallDuration,0.25,4);
      to=Math.min(end,start+Math.max(0.65,cycle));
    }
    from=Math.max(start,Math.min(end-0.04,from));
    to=Math.max(from+0.04,Math.min(end,to));
    const stopAt=Math.max(from,to-0.001);

    // Keep the shared timeline long enough even in projects with no character at all.
    if(typeof timelineDuration!=='undefined') timelineDuration=Math.max(Number(timelineDuration)||0,end);

    // Stop a previous preview, but keep the current media clip selected in the timeline.
    if(isPlaying) pausePlayback();
    if(clipAnimationPreviewRaf) cancelAnimationFrame(clipAnimationPreviewRaf);
    clipAnimationPreviewRaf=0;
    const token=++clipAnimationPreviewToken;

    seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds(from);
    isPlaying=true;
    playbackStartedAtMs=performance.now();
    playbackStartTimeSec=from;
    const icon=document.getElementById('playIcon');
    if(icon) icon.innerHTML='<path d="M6 5h4v14H6zM14 5h4v14h-4z"/>';

    const tick=(timestamp)=>{
      if(token!==clipAnimationPreviewToken || !isPlaying) return;
      const t=playbackStartTimeSec+(timestamp-playbackStartedAtMs)/1000;
      if(t>=to){
        // Out ends on an empty/hidden clip, so park the playhead where the tail started (clip visible).
        setCurrentAbsoluteTime(category==='out'?from:stopAt);
        isPlaying=false;
        clipAnimationPreviewRaf=0;
        playbackRaf=null;
        const playIcon=document.getElementById('playIcon');
        if(playIcon) playIcon.innerHTML='<path d="M8 5v14l11-7z"/>';
        renderClipAnimationPreviewFrame();
        return;
      }
      setCurrentAbsoluteTime(t);
      renderClipAnimationPreviewFrame();
      clipAnimationPreviewRaf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      playbackRaf=clipAnimationPreviewRaf;
    };
    clipAnimationPreviewRaf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    playbackRaf=clipAnimationPreviewRaf;
  }

  function updateClipPanelRangeProgress(input){
    if(!input || input.type!=='range') return;
    const min=Number(input.min)||0;
    const max=Number(input.max)||100;
    const value=Number(input.value)||0;
    const pct=max<=min?0:Math.max(0,Math.min(100,(value-min)/(max-min)*100));
    input.style.setProperty('--ev-range-progress',pct+'%');
  }
  function initClipPanelRanges(root){
    if(!root) return;
    root.querySelectorAll('.evca-panel input[type="range"]').forEach(updateClipPanelRangeProgress);
  }
  document.addEventListener('input',function(e){
    if(e.target && e.target.matches && e.target.matches('.evca-panel input[type="range"]')) updateClipPanelRangeProgress(e.target);
  },true);

  function schedulePersist(){
    clearTimeout(panelPersistTimer);
    panelPersistTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
      try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle filter/animation save:',e);}
    },220);
  }
  function commitModelChange(model){
    try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle save:',e);}
  }
  const animationOptions={
    in:[['none','None'],['fade','Fade'],['zoom-in','Zoom in'],['zoom-out','Zoom out'],['slide-left','Slide left'],['slide-right','Slide right'],['slide-up','Slide up'],['slide-down','Slide down'],['pop','Pop'],['rotate','Rotate'],['bounce','Bounce'],['elastic','Elastic'],['spin','Spin'],['swirl','Swirl'],['swing','Swing'],['wobble','Wobble'],['drop','Drop'],['rise','Rise'],['fade-left','Fade left'],['fade-right','Fade right'],['fade-up','Fade up'],['fade-down','Fade down'],['whip','Whip'],['grow','Grow'],['glitch','Glitch'],['flicker','Flicker'],['zoom-fade','Zoom fade'],['tilt-left','Tilt left'],['tilt-right','Tilt right'],['roll-left','Roll left'],['roll-right','Roll right'],['tada','Ta-da'],['rubber','Rubber band'],['zoom-spin','Zoom spin'],['slam','Slam'],['zoom-bounce','Zoom bounce'],['tumble','Tumble'],['zigzag','Zigzag'],['launch','Launch'],['shiver','Shiver'],['diagonal','Diagonal'],['orbit','Orbit']],
    out:[['none','None'],['fade','Fade out'],['zoom-in','Zoom out'],['zoom-out','Zoom in'],['slide-left','Slide right'],['slide-right','Slide left'],['slide-up','Slide down'],['slide-down','Slide up'],['pop','Pop out'],['rotate','Rotate out'],['bounce','Bounce out'],['elastic','Elastic out'],['spin','Spin out'],['swirl','Swirl out'],['swing','Swing out'],['wobble','Wobble out'],['drop','Rise'],['rise','Drop'],['fade-left','Fade right'],['fade-right','Fade left'],['fade-up','Fade down'],['fade-down','Fade up'],['whip','Whip out'],['grow','Shrink'],['glitch','Glitch out'],['flicker','Flicker out'],['zoom-fade','Zoom fade out'],['tilt-left','Tilt left out'],['tilt-right','Tilt right out'],['roll-left','Roll left out'],['roll-right','Roll right out'],['tada','Ta-da out'],['rubber','Rubber band out'],['zoom-spin','Zoom spin out'],['slam','Slam out'],['zoom-bounce','Zoom bounce out'],['tumble','Tumble out'],['zigzag','Zigzag out'],['launch','Launch out'],['shiver','Shiver out'],['diagonal','Diagonal out'],['orbit','Orbit out']],
    overall:[['none','None'],['pulse','Pulse'],['float','Float'],['shake','Shake'],['zoom','Zoom'],['rotate','Rotate'],['heartbeat','Heartbeat'],['sway','Sway'],['jelly','Jelly'],['spin-loop','Spin loop'],['wiggle','Wiggle'],['bob','Bob'],['breathe','Breathe'],['flash','Flash']]
  };
  /* v411: Overall gets 7 more loops. In / Out / Overall keep their own lists, nothing leaks across tabs. */
  [['pendulum','Pendulum'],['drift','Drift'],['tremble','Tremble'],['hop','Hop'],['glow','Glow'],['tilt','Tilt'],['swell','Swell']]
    .forEach(e=>{if(!animationOptions.overall.some(x=>x[0]===e[0]))animationOptions.overall.push(e);});
  /* v411: preview tiles are driven by the SAME functions the clip uses (phaseState / applyOverallMotion),
     so a tile moves exactly like the animation it applies, and In tiles play in, Out tiles play out. */
  let clipTileRaf=0;
  function startClipTilePreview(){
    if(clipTileRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(clipTileRaf);clipTileRaf=0;}
    const root=document.getElementById('clipAnimSettingsBody');if(!root)return;
    const cat=animCategory;
    const tiles=Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('.evca-preset')).map((btn,i)=>({key:btn.dataset.preset,sprite:btn.querySelector('.evca-preview-sprite'),off:(i%7)*0.09})).filter(x=>x.sprite&&x.key&&x.key!=='none');
    if(!tiles.length)return;
    const W=160,H=90,SC=.28,t0=performance.now();let last=0;
    function frame(now){
      if(!root.isConnected||animCategory!==cat){clipTileRaf=0;return;}
      clipTileRaf=requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      if(!root.offsetParent||now-last<33)return;
      last=now;
      const s=(now-t0)/1000;
      tiles.forEach(tl=>{
        let st={opacity:1,scale:1,x:0,y:0,rotation:0};
        try{
          const u=s+tl.off;
          if(cat==='overall'){const period=1.2;applyOverallMotion(st,tl.key,u,period,((u%period)/period)*Math.PI*2,W,H);}
          else if(cat==='in'){const c=u%2.1;st=phaseState(tl.key,'in',c<1?c:1,W,H);}
          else{const c=u%2.1;st=phaseState(tl.key,'out',c<1.1?0:clamp(c-1.1,0,1),W,H);}
        }catch(_e){}
        tl.sprite.style.opacity=String(clamp(st.opacity,0,1));
        tl.sprite.style.transform='translate('+(st.x*SC).toFixed(1)+'px,'+(st.y*SC).toFixed(1)+'px) rotate('+st.rotation.toFixed(1)+'deg) scale('+clamp(st.scale,0.05,3).toFixed(3)+')';
      });
    }
    clipTileRaf=requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationCategory=function(cat){
    if(!animationOptions[cat])return;
    if(isPlaying) pausePlayback();
    animCategory=cat;
    clipAnimationSearchQuery='';
    renderClipAnimationPanel();
    const root=document.getElementById('clipAnimSettingsBody');
    const activeTab=root&&root.querySelector('.evca-tab.active');
    if(activeTab){
      activeTab.classList.add('evca-flash-selected');
      setTimeout(()=>{if(activeTab.isConnected)activeTab.classList.remove('evca-flash-selected');},500);
    }
  };
  window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationPreset=function(preset){
    const ov=overlayForSelected();if(!ov||!isVisualModel(ov))return;
    const c=getAnimationConfig(ov);c[animCategory]=preset;
    if(animCategory==='in' && c.in!=='none' && c.inDuration==null)c.inDuration=0.45;
    commitModelChange(ov);
    renderClipAnimationPanel();
    if(preset!=='none'){
      try{previewClipAnimationWindow(ov,animCategory);}
      catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle animation playback preview:',e);}
    }else{
      try{if(isPlaying)pausePlayback();seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds(Math.max(0,Number(ov.trimStart)||0));}
      catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle animation reset preview:',e);}
    }
  };
  window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationDuration=function(value){
    const ov=overlayForSelected();if(!ov||!isVisualModel(ov))return;
    const c=getAnimationConfig(ov);const key=animCategory==='overall'?'overallDuration':animCategory+'Duration';
    c[key]=clamp(value,animCategory==='overall'?0.25:0.1,animCategory==='overall'?4:2.5);
    const output=document.getElementById('evca-duration-value');if(output)output.textContent=Number(c[key]).toFixed(2)+'s';
    schedulePersist();
  };
  function clipPresetPreviewMarkup(key){
    // Use the supplied Ethvelle animation artwork in every preset tile. The outer sprite
    // still receives each preset's own CSS motion so the previews remain individually distinct.
    return `<span class="evca-preview-scene" aria-hidden="true"><span class="evca-preview-sprite"><img src="https://i.postimg.cc/sgnfkvT3/Gemini-Generated-Image-ld9tt5ld9tt5ld9t.jpg" alt="" draggable="false" loading="eager" decoding="async"></span></span>`;
  }
  let clipAnimationSearchQuery='';
  window.filterClipAnimationPresets=function(value){
    clipAnimationSearchQuery=String(value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    const wrap=document.getElementById('clipAnimationSearchWrap');if(wrap)wrap.classList.toggle('has-query',!!clipAnimationSearchQuery);
    const root=document.getElementById('clipAnimSettingsBody');if(!root)return;
    let visible=0;root.querySelectorAll('.evca-preset').forEach(btn=>{const label=String(btn.querySelector('.evca-preset-label')?.textContent||'').toLowerCase(),key=String(btn.dataset.preset||'').toLowerCase(),show=!clipAnimationSearchQuery||label.includes(clipAnimationSearchQuery)||key.includes(clipAnimationSearchQuery);btn.hidden=!show;if(show)visible++;});
    const empty=root.querySelector('#evca-animation-no-results');if(empty)empty.hidden=visible>0;
  };
  window.clearClipAnimationPresetSearch=function(){const input=document.getElementById('clipAnimationSearchInput');if(input)input.value='';window.filterClipAnimationPresets('');if(input)input.focus();};
  function renderClipAnimationPanel(){
    const root=document.getElementById('clipAnimSettingsBody');if(!root)return;
    const ov=overlayForSelected();
    if(!ov||!isVisualModel(ov)){
      root.innerHTML='<p class="evca-panel-note">Select an image, video, or text clip on the timeline to add clip animations.</p>';return;
    }
    const c=getAnimationConfig(ov);const options=animationOptions[animCategory];const chosen=c[animCategory]||'none';
    const durationKey=animCategory==='overall'?'overallDuration':animCategory+'Duration';
    const duration=c[durationKey] == null ? (animCategory==='overall'?1.2:0.45) : c[durationKey];
    const categoryLabel=animCategory==='in'?'In animation':animCategory==='out'?'Out animation':'Overall animation';
    const shownOptions=options.filter(([key,label])=>!clipAnimationSearchQuery||key.toLowerCase().includes(clipAnimationSearchQuery)||label.toLowerCase().includes(clipAnimationSearchQuery));
    root.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-panel">
      <div class="evca-tabs">
        <button type="button" class="evca-tab ${animCategory==='in'?'active':''}" onclick="window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationCategory('in')">In animation</button>
        <button type="button" class="evca-tab ${animCategory==='out'?'active':''}" onclick="window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationCategory('out')">Out animation</button>
        <button type="button" class="evca-tab ${animCategory==='overall'?'active':''}" onclick="window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationCategory('overall')">Overall animation</button>
      </div>
      <div class="editor-asset-search-wrap evca-search-wrap${clipAnimationSearchQuery?' has-query':''}" id="clipAnimationSearchWrap">
        <svg class="editor-asset-search-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"></circle><path d="m16 16 4.2 4.2"></path></svg>
        <input class="editor-asset-search" id="clipAnimationSearchInput" type="search" aria-label="Search clip animations" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search animations…" value="${escapeHtml(clipAnimationSearchQuery)}" oninput="window.filterClipAnimationPresets(this.value)">
        <button type="button" class="editor-asset-search-clear" aria-label="Clear animation search" onclick="window.clearClipAnimationPresetSearch()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 7l10 10M17 7L7 17"/></svg></button>
      </div>
      <div><div class="evca-section-title">${categoryLabel}</div><div class="evca-preset-grid" data-category="${animCategory}">${shownOptions.map(([key,label])=>`<button type="button" class="evca-preset ${chosen===key?'active':''}" data-preset="${key}" data-animation-category="${animCategory}" aria-pressed="${chosen===key?'true':'false'}" onclick="window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationPreset('${key}')">${clipPresetPreviewMarkup(key)}<span class="evca-preset-label">${label}</span></button>`).join('')}</div><p class="evca-panel-note" id="evca-animation-no-results" ${shownOptions.length?'hidden':''}>No animations found. Try another search.</p></div>
      <label class="evpa-range evca-range-control"><span>${animCategory==='overall'?'Cycle duration':'Duration'}<output id="evca-duration-value">${Number(duration).toFixed(2)}s</output></span><input type="range" min="${animCategory==='overall'?'0.25':'0.1'}" max="${animCategory==='overall'?'4':'2.5'}" step="0.05" value="${duration}" oninput="window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationDuration(this.value)" onchange="window.ethvellePreviewSelectedClipAnimation()"></label>
    </div>`;
    initClipPanelRanges(root);
    startClipTilePreview();
  }
  window.ethvellePreviewSelectedClipAnimation=function(){
    const ov=overlayForSelected();
    if(!ov||!isVisualModel(ov))return;
    try{previewClipAnimationWindow(ov,animCategory);}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle animation duration preview:',e);}
  };

  let clipFilterSearchQuery='';
  window.filterClipFilterPresets=function(value){
    clipFilterSearchQuery=String(value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    const wrap=document.getElementById('clipFilterSearchWrap');if(wrap)wrap.classList.toggle('has-query',!!clipFilterSearchQuery);
    const root=document.getElementById('clipFilterSettingsBody');if(!root)return;
    let visible=0;root.querySelectorAll('.evca-filter-preset').forEach(btn=>{const text=String(btn.textContent||'').toLowerCase();const show=!clipFilterSearchQuery||text.includes(clipFilterSearchQuery);btn.hidden=!show;if(show)visible++;});
    const empty=root.querySelector('#evca-filter-no-results');if(empty)empty.hidden=visible>0;
  };
  window.clearClipFilterPresetSearch=function(){const input=document.getElementById('clipFilterSearchInput');if(input)input.value='';window.filterClipFilterPresets('');if(input)input.focus();};
  const filterPresets={
    original:{label:'Original',adjustments:{...defaults}},
    vivid:{label:'Vivid',adjustments:{brightness:103,contrast:112,saturation:142,hue:0,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:0}},
    warm:{label:'Warm',adjustments:{brightness:103,contrast:104,saturation:115,hue:-9,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:18}},
    cool:{label:'Cool',adjustments:{brightness:102,contrast:108,saturation:108,hue:18,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:0}},
    bw:{label:'B&W',adjustments:{brightness:100,contrast:112,saturation:100,hue:0,blur:0,grayscale:100,sepia:0}},
    sepia:{label:'Sepia',adjustments:{brightness:101,contrast:105,saturation:82,hue:0,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:72}},
    dramatic:{label:'Dramatic',adjustments:{brightness:96,contrast:132,saturation:88,hue:0,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:0}}
  };
  function normalizeAdjustments(obj){return Object.assign({},defaults,obj||{});}
  function applySelectedFilterPreview(target){
    if(!target||!target.model)return;
    const model=target.model;
    const filter=window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter?window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(model.adjustments,model.enhanceAmount||0):'none';
    if(target.kind==='overlay'){
      const id=String(model.id);
      const base=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[id]:null;
      const keyed=model.type==='video'&&model.chromaKey&&model.chromaKey.enabled&&typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'?overlayChromaCanvases[id]:null;
      const el=keyed||base;
      if(el&&el.style.filter!==filter)el.style.filter=filter;
    }else{
      const img=document.getElementById('bgImgLayer');
      const video=document.getElementById('bgVideoLayer');
      if(img&&img.style.filter!==filter)img.style.filter=filter;
      if(video&&video.style.filter!==filter)video.style.filter=filter;
    }
  }
  window.ethvelleSetClipFilterPreset=function(key){
    const target=currentVisualTarget();if(!target)return;
    const preset=filterPresets[key];if(!preset)return;
    target.model.adjustments={...preset.adjustments};target.model.filterPreset=key;
    target.model.enhanceAmount=0;target.model.__evltEnhanceUserSet=true;
    applySelectedFilterPreview(target);
    try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle filter save:',e);}
    renderClipFilterPanel();
  };
  window.ethvelleSetClipFilterValue=function(key,value,rangeEl){
    const target=currentVisualTarget();if(!target||!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(defaults,key))return;
    const limits={brightness:[0,200],contrast:[0,200],saturation:[0,200],hue:[-180,180],blur:[0,12],grayscale:[0,100],sepia:[0,100]};
    if(!target.model.adjustments)target.model.adjustments={...defaults};
    target.model.adjustments[key]=clamp(value,limits[key][0],limits[key][1]);target.model.filterPreset='custom';target.model.enhanceAmount=0;target.model.__evltEnhanceUserSet=true;
    const out=document.getElementById('evf-value-'+key);if(out)out.textContent=String(target.model.adjustments[key])+(key==='hue'?'°':key==='blur'?'px':'%');
    if(rangeEl)rangeEl.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',String(target.model.adjustments[key]));
    applySelectedFilterPreview(target);schedulePersist();
  };
  function renderClipFilterPanel(){
    const root=document.getElementById('clipFilterSettingsBody');if(!root)return;
    const target=currentVisualTarget();
    if(!target){root.innerHTML='<p class="evca-panel-note">Select an image, video, text, or Background clip to apply a filter.</p>';return;}
    const model=target.model;const adj=normalizeAdjustments(model.adjustments);const active=model.filterPreset||'original';
    const sliderDefs=[
      ['brightness','Brightness',0,200,1,'%'],['contrast','Contrast',0,200,1,'%'],['saturation','Saturation',0,200,1,'%'],
      ['hue','Hue',-180,180,1,'°'],['blur','Blur',0,12,0.5,'px'],['grayscale','B&W',0,100,1,'%'],['sepia','Sepia',0,100,1,'%']
    ];
    const shownFilters=Object.entries(filterPresets).filter(([key,p])=>!clipFilterSearchQuery||key.toLowerCase().includes(clipFilterSearchQuery)||p.label.toLowerCase().includes(clipFilterSearchQuery));
    root.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-panel">
      <div><div class="evca-section-title">Presets</div>
        <div class="editor-asset-search-wrap evca-search-wrap${clipFilterSearchQuery?' has-query':''}" id="clipFilterSearchWrap">
          <svg class="editor-asset-search-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"></circle><path d="m16 16 4.2 4.2"></path></svg>
          <input class="editor-asset-search" id="clipFilterSearchInput" type="search" aria-label="Search filters" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search filters…" value="${escapeHtml(clipFilterSearchQuery)}" oninput="window.filterClipFilterPresets(this.value)">
          <button type="button" class="editor-asset-search-clear" aria-label="Clear filter search" onclick="window.clearClipFilterPresetSearch()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 7l10 10M17 7L7 17"/></svg></button>
        </div>
        <div class="evca-filter-presets">${shownFilters.map(([key,p])=>`<button type="button" class="evca-filter-preset ${active===key?'active':''}" onclick="window.ethvelleSetClipFilterPreset('${key}')"><span class="evca-filter-swatch ${key==='original'?'':key}"></span><span>${p.label}</span></button>`).join('')}</div>
        <p class="evca-panel-note" id="evca-filter-no-results" ${shownFilters.length?'hidden':''}>No filters found. Try another search.</p>
      </div>
      <div>${sliderDefs.map(([key,label,min,max,step,suffix])=>`<label class="evpa-range evca-range-control"><span>${label}<output id="evf-value-${key}">${Number(adj[key])}${suffix}</output></span><input id="evf-range-${key}" type="range" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}" value="${Number(adj[key])}" oninput="window.ethvelleSetClipFilterValue('${key}',this.value,this)"></label>`).join('')}</div>
      <button class="evca-filter-reset" type="button" onclick="window.ethvelleSetClipFilterPreset('original')">Reset filter</button>
    </div>`;
    initClipPanelRanges(root);
  }

  // Extension panels use the editor's established slide-up panel rather than adding a
  // second modal or replacing the existing panel-routing logic.
  try{
    if(typeof panelContent!=='undefined'){
      panelContent.clipAnimations={title:'Clip animations',body:'<div id="clipAnimSettingsBody"></div>'};
      panelContent.clipFilter={title:'Filter',body:'<div id="clipFilterSettingsBody"></div>'};
    }
  }catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle panel registration:',e);}
  const oldOpenPanel=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpenPanel==='function'&&!oldOpenPanel.__ethvelleV30Panels){
    const wrappedOpenPanel=function(key){
      const result=oldOpenPanel.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='clipAnimations')renderClipAnimationPanel();
      if(key==='clipFilter')renderClipFilterPanel();
      return result;
    };
    wrappedOpenPanel.__ethvelleV30Panels=true;window.openPanel=wrappedOpenPanel;
  }

  function addToolbarTool(id,label,svg,handler){
    const row=document.getElementById('bottomToolsClip');if(!row||document.getElementById(id))return;
    const tool=document.createElement('div');tool.className='tool-item evca-toolbar-tool';tool.id=id;tool.setAttribute('role','button');tool.setAttribute('tabindex','0');tool.setAttribute('aria-label',label);tool.style.display='none';
    tool.innerHTML=svg+'<span>'+label+'</span>';
    tool.addEventListener('click',handler);tool.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();handler();}});
    const before=document.getElementById('clipTrimTool')||document.getElementById('clipForwardTool');if(before&&before.parentNode===row)row.insertBefore(tool,before);else row.appendChild(tool);
  }
  function bootToolbar(){
    const animSvg='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 16.5 8.5 12 4 7.5"/><path d="M20 7.5 15.5 12 20 16.5"/><path d="M9 5h6M9 19h6"/><path d="M12 5v14"/></svg>';
    const filterSvg='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 5h16M7 12h10M10 19h4"/><circle cx="8" cy="5" r="2" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/><circle cx="15" cy="12" r="2" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/><circle cx="12" cy="19" r="2" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/></svg>';
    addToolbarTool('clipOverlayAnimationTool','Clip animations',animSvg,()=>window.openPanel('clipAnimations'));
    addToolbarTool('clipOverlayFilterTool','Filter',filterSvg,()=>window.openPanel('clipFilter'));
    const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
    if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__ethvelleV30Toolbar){
      const wrappedSync=function(){
        const r=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);
        const a=document.getElementById('clipOverlayAnimationTool'),f=document.getElementById('clipOverlayFilterTool');
        const ov=overlayForSelected(),bg=backgroundForSelected();
        const canAnimate=!!ov&&isVisualModel(ov);
        const canFilter=(!!ov&&visualTypes.has(ov.type))||!!bg;
        if(a)a.style.display=canAnimate?'flex':'none';
        if(f)f.style.display=canFilter?'flex':'none';
        return r;
      };
      wrappedSync.__ethvelleV30Toolbar=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrappedSync;
    }
    try{if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();}catch(e){}
  }

  // Apply the chosen animation + filter after the base renderer has updated its DOM elements.
  // This keeps animation state independent of clip selection and avoids rewriting the core
  // compositor, while the export path below uses the same state function for frame parity.
  const oldRenderOverlayMedia=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof oldRenderOverlayMedia==='function'&&!oldRenderOverlayMedia.__ethvelleV30Animations){
    const wrappedRenderOverlayMedia=function(tSecAbsolute){
      const result=oldRenderOverlayMedia.apply(this,arguments);
      try{
        const t=Number.isFinite(Number(tSecAbsolute))?Number(tSecAbsolute):(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec==='number'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0);
        getOverlays().forEach(ov=>{
          if(!ov||!visualTypes.has(ov.type))return;
          let el=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[ov.id]:null;
          if(!el)return;
          const keyCanvas=ov.type==='video'&&ov.chromaKey&&ov.chromaKey.enabled&&typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'?overlayChromaCanvases[ov.id]:null;
          const target=keyCanvas||el;
          const tr=window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(ov):(ov.transform||{});const a=window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(ov,t);
          const transform=`translate(${(Number(tr.offsetX)||0)+a.x}px, ${(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)+a.y}px) rotate(${(Number(tr.rotation)||0)+a.rotation}deg) scale(${(Number(tr.scale)||1)*a.scale})`+(window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix?window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix(ov):'');
          if(target.style.transform!==transform)target.style.transform=transform;
          const rawAnimationOpacity=Number(a.opacity==null?1:a.opacity);
          const animationOpacity=Number.isFinite(rawAnimationOpacity)?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,rawAnimationOpacity)):1;
          const rawTextOpacity=Number(ov.textOverallOpacity==null?100:ov.textOverallOpacity);
          const textOpacityMultiplier=ov.type==='text'?(Number.isFinite(rawTextOpacity)?Math.max(0,Math.min(100,rawTextOpacity))/100:1):1;
          const combinedOpacity=animationOpacity*textOpacityMultiplier;
          if(target.style.opacity!==String(combinedOpacity))target.style.opacity=String(combinedOpacity);
          const filter=window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter?window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(ov.adjustments,ov.enhanceAmount||0):'none';
          if(target.style.filter!==filter)target.style.filter=filter;
          if(keyCanvas&&el.style.opacity!=='0')el.style.opacity='0';
        });
      }catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle clip animation preview:',e);}
      return result;
    };
    wrappedRenderOverlayMedia.__ethvelleV30Animations=true;window.renderOverlayMedia=wrappedRenderOverlayMedia;
  }
  const oldApplyBackground=window.applyBackground;
  if(typeof oldApplyBackground==='function'&&!oldApplyBackground.__ethvelleV30Filters){
    const wrappedApplyBackground=function(){
      const result=oldApplyBackground.apply(this,arguments);
      try{
        const bg=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec==='number'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0):backgroundForSelected();
        const img=document.getElementById('bgImgLayer');
        if(bg&&img){const filter=window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter?window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(bg.adjustments,bg.enhanceAmount||0):'none';if(img.style.filter!==filter)img.style.filter=filter;}
      }catch(e){}
      return result;
    };
    wrappedApplyBackground.__ethvelleV30Filters=true;window.applyBackground=wrappedApplyBackground;
  }
  try{bootToolbar();}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle clip toolbar setup:',e);}
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v32-project-open-restore-guard">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const original=window.openEditor;
  if(typeof original!=='function'||original.__evV32RestoreGuard)return;
  function latestProject(project){
    if(!project||project.id==null)return project;
    try{
      const list=(typeof projects!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(projects))?projects:[];
      const latest=list.find(item=>String(item&&item.id)===String(project.id));
      if(latest && !(latest.__evIndexOnly && !project.__evIndexOnly))return latest;
      if(latest && project.__evIndexOnly)return latest;
    }catch(_e){}
    return project;
  }
  const wrapped=function(project){
    const args=Array.from(arguments),ctx=this;
    const restore=window.__ethvelleProjectsRestorePromise;
    const openNow=()=>{args[0]=latestProject(args[0]);return original.apply(ctx,args);};
    if(restore && !window.__ethvelleProjectsRestoreReady){
      // Never open an early localStorage index entry while the full savedState/media refs are
      // still being restored from IndexedDB. Otherwise the editor starts with an empty overlays[].
      return restore.then(openNow,openNow);
    }
    return openNow();
  };
  wrapped.__evV32RestoreGuard=true;
  window.openEditor=wrapped;
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v42-focus-and-short-clip-fix">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const SHORT_CLIP_WIDTH = 42;
  const CLIP_SELECTOR = '#timelineInner .anim-clip, #timelineInner .overlay-clip, #timelineInner .track-clip';
  const observedClips = new WeakSet();
  let timelineRoot = null;
  let timelineMutationObserver = null;
  let bodyWaitObserver = null;
  let clipResizeObserver = null;

  function updateShortClip(el){
    if(!el || !el.isConnected) return;
    const width = el.getBoundingClientRect().width || Number.parseFloat(el.style.width) || 0;
    el.classList.toggle('ev-short-clip', width > 0 && width < SHORT_CLIP_WIDTH);
  }
  function observeClip(el){
    if(!el || !el.matches || !el.matches('.anim-clip, .overlay-clip, .track-clip')) return;
    updateShortClip(el);
    if(clipResizeObserver && !observedClips.has(el)){
      observedClips.add(el);
      try{clipResizeObserver.observe(el);}catch(_e){}
    }
  }
  function observeAdded(node){
    if(!node || node.nodeType !== 1) return;
    if(node.matches && node.matches('.anim-clip, .overlay-clip, .track-clip')) observeClip(node);
    if(node.querySelectorAll) node.querySelectorAll('.anim-clip, .overlay-clip, .track-clip').forEach(observeClip);
  }
  function attachTimelineObservers(){
    const root = document.getElementById('timelineInner');
    if(!root || root === timelineRoot) return;
    if(timelineMutationObserver) timelineMutationObserver.disconnect();
    timelineRoot = root;
    if(typeof ResizeObserver === 'function'){
      clipResizeObserver = clipResizeObserver || new ResizeObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(entry=>updateShortClip(entry.target)));
    }
    root.querySelectorAll('.anim-clip, .overlay-clip, .track-clip').forEach(observeClip);
    if(typeof MutationObserver === 'function'){
      timelineMutationObserver = new MutationObserver(records=>records.forEach(record=>record.addedNodes.forEach(observeAdded)));
      timelineMutationObserver.observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    }
    if(bodyWaitObserver){bodyWaitObserver.disconnect();bodyWaitObserver=null;}
  }
  if(document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', attachTimelineObservers, {once:true});
  else attachTimelineObservers();
  if(document.body && !timelineRoot && typeof MutationObserver === 'function'){
    bodyWaitObserver = new MutationObserver(attachTimelineObservers);
    bodyWaitObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{if(timelineRoot)timelineRoot.querySelectorAll('.anim-clip, .overlay-clip, .track-clip').forEach(updateShortClip);},{passive:true});

  /* Preserve the composing text input focus while applying a swatch. This keeps the software
     keyboard available when a user picks a text colour rather than forcing them to tap the text
     field again after every colour change. */
  let focusToRestore = null;
  let palettePointerDown = false;
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const inPalette = !!(e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest(
      '#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette button, #ethvelleTextComposer #evtcColorStrip button, #ethvelleTextComposer .evpa-palette button'
    ));
    palettePointerDown = inPalette;
    focusToRestore = null;
    if(!inPalette) return;
    const active = document.activeElement;
    if(active && active.matches && active.matches('textarea, input:not([type="range"]), [contenteditable="true"]') && active.closest('#ethvelleTextComposer')){
      focusToRestore = active;
    }
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>{
    const input = palettePointerDown ? focusToRestore : null;
    palettePointerDown = false;
    focusToRestore = null;
    if(!input) return;
    setTimeout(()=>{
      const composer = document.getElementById('ethvelleTextComposer');
      if(!input.isConnected || !composer || !composer.classList.contains('active')) return;
      if(document.activeElement !== input){
        try{input.focus({preventScroll:true});}catch(_e){try{input.focus();}catch(__e){}}
      }
    },90);
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{palettePointerDown=false;focusToRestore=null;},true);
})();
</script>
