<script id="ethvelle-v43-text-picker-timeline-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const norm = v => {
    const s=String(v||'').trim();
    if(/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(s))return s.toUpperCase();
    const m=s.match(/rgba?\((\d+)[, ]+\s*(\d+)[, ]+\s*(\d+)/i);
    return m?'#'+m.slice(1,4).map(n=>Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Number(n))).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase():'';
  };
  let composerColor='';
  function syncComposerSwatches(color){
    const c=norm(color);if(!c)return;composerColor=c;
    const strip=document.getElementById('evtcColorStrip');if(!strip)return;
    strip.querySelectorAll('.evtc-color-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',norm(b.dataset.color)===c));
  }
  window.__ethvelleSyncTextSwatches=function(key,color){
    const c=norm(color);if(!c)return;
    if(key==='textColor')syncComposerSwatches(c);
    const palette=document.querySelector('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette');
    if(palette){
      palette.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',norm(b.dataset.color)===c));
    }
    const stylePanel=document.getElementById('ethvelleTextStylePanel');
    if(stylePanel){stylePanel.querySelectorAll('.evts-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',norm(b.dataset.color)===c));}
  };

  /* Wrap, rather than replace, the existing setters so live application and project saving stay intact. */
  const oldComposerSet=window.setEthvelleComposerColor;
  if(typeof oldComposerSet==='function'&&!oldComposerSet.__ev43StableSelection){
    const wrapped=function(color){const out=oldComposerSet.apply(this,arguments);syncComposerSwatches(color);return out;};
    wrapped.__ev43StableSelection=true;window.setEthvelleComposerColor=wrapped;
  }
  const oldDockSet=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
  if(typeof oldDockSet==='function'&&!oldDockSet.__ev43StableSelection){
    const wrapped=function(key,color){const out=oldDockSet.apply(this,arguments);window.__ethvelleSyncTextSwatches(key,color);return out;};
    wrapped.__ev43StableSelection=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=wrapped;
  }
  const oldComposerOpen=window.openEthvelleTextComposer;
  if(typeof oldComposerOpen==='function'&&!oldComposerOpen.__ev43StableSelection){
    const wrapped=function(){const out=oldComposerOpen.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      const input=document.getElementById('evtcStageText');
      const target=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;
      syncComposerSwatches(target?.textColor||input?.style.color||'#FFFFFF');
    });return out;};
    wrapped.__ev43StableSelection=true;window.openEthvelleTextComposer=wrapped;
  }

  /* Avoid moving focus to a colour button while the text field owns the Android keyboard.
     Preventing the pointer's default focus action prevents the keyboard close/reopen flash;
     the button's normal click/onClick handler still applies the colour. */
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const button=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest(
      '#ethvelleTextComposer #evtcColorStrip .evtc-color-swatch, #ethvelleTextComposer #evtcColorStrip .evtc-swatch-action, #ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-swatch, #ethvelleTextStylePanel .evts-swatch'
    ):null;
    if(!button)return;
    const active=document.activeElement;
    if(active&&active.matches&&active.matches('textarea,input:not([type="range"]),[contenteditable="true"]')&&active.closest('#ethvelleTextComposer')){
      e.preventDefault();
    }
  },{capture:true,passive:false});

  /* Keep the composer ring correct if a palette rebuild recreates its swatch nodes. */
  const composer=document.getElementById('ethvelleTextComposer');
  if(composer&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    const strip=composer.querySelector('#evtcColorStrip');
    if(strip&&!strip.dataset.ev43Observed){strip.dataset.ev43Observed='1';new MutationObserver(()=>{if(composerColor)syncComposerSwatches(composerColor);}).observe(strip,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','data-color']});}
  }

  /* Add Text behaves like timeline insertion: the first text clip begins at 0s. Later text clips
     insert before the nearest clip when the playhead is in its first half, or after it in the
     second half; following text clips move forward rather than overlapping. */
  window.ethvellePlanTextInsertStart=function(duration){
    const list=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:(Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[]);
    const len=Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,Number(duration)||5);
    const clips=list.filter(o=>o&&o.type==='text'&&Number.isFinite(Number(o.trimStart))&&Number.isFinite(Number(o.trimEnd))&&Number(o.trimEnd)>Number(o.trimStart))
      .sort((a,b)=>Number(a.trimStart)-Number(b.trimStart));
    if(!clips.length)return 0;
    const playhead=Math.max(0,Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec))||0);
    let anchor=clips.find(c=>playhead>=Number(c.trimStart)-.001&&playhead<Number(c.trimEnd)-.001);
    if(!anchor){
      let best=Infinity;
      clips.forEach(c=>{const st=Number(c.trimStart),en=Number(c.trimEnd),d=playhead<st?st-playhead:(playhead>en?playhead-en:0);if(d<best){best=d;anchor=c;}});
    }
    const st=Math.max(0,Number(anchor.trimStart)||0),en=Math.max(st+MIN_CLIP_SEC,Number(anchor.trimEnd)||st+len);
    const insertAfter=playhead>=(st+en)/2;
    const insertAt=insertAfter?en:st;
    clips.forEach(c=>{
      if(Number(c.trimEnd)>insertAt+.0001){
        const oldStart=Number(c.trimStart)||0;
        const oldKeyframeTimes=Array.isArray(c.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)?c.ethvelleMediaKeyframes.map(k=>Number(k&&k.t)||0):[];
        c.trimStart=oldStart+len;c.trimEnd=Number(c.trimEnd)+len;
        shiftTimelineKeyframesOnce(c.ethvelleMediaKeyframes,oldKeyframeTimes,len);
      }
    });
    try{if(typeof timelineDuration!=='undefined')timelineDuration=Math.max(Number(timelineDuration)||0,insertAt+len,...clips.map(c=>Number(c.trimEnd)||0));}catch(_e){}
    return insertAt;
  };
})();
</script>



<!-- Ethvelle v45: reliable text colour taps, stable swatch state, mobile recorder reliability,
     exact before/after preview alignment, and hide on-canvas selection chrome during Enhance. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v45-text-enhance-repair-css">
  /* Inactive swatches explicitly have no blue ring; only the currently applied colour can show it. */
  #ethvelleTextComposer #evtcColorStrip .evtc-color-swatch:not(.active),
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-swatch:not(.active),
  #ethvelleTextStylePanel .evts-swatch:not(.active) {
    border-color:transparent!important;outline-color:transparent!important;box-shadow:none!important;
  }
  #ethvelleTextComposer #evtcColorStrip .evtc-color-swatch.active,
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-swatch.active,
  #ethvelleTextStylePanel .evts-swatch.active {
    border:2px solid #2F5CE8!important;outline:2px solid #2F5CE8!important;outline-offset:1px!important;
    box-shadow:0 0 0 2px #15161E,0 0 0 3px #2F5CE8!important;position:relative!important;z-index:30!important;
  }
  /* The on-canvas transform frame is editing chrome, not part of the enhancement preview. */
  body.evlt-enhance-preview-active #selBoxLayer,
  body.evlt-enhance-preview-active #selBoxLayer .sb-frame,
  body.evlt-enhance-preview-active #selBoxLayer .sb-btn {display:none!important;visibility:hidden!important;pointer-events:none!important;}
  body.evlt-enhance-preview-active #ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot,
  body.evlt-enhance-preview-active #ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot .evcc-layer,
  body.evlt-enhance-preview-active #ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot .evcc-media {box-sizing:border-box!important;}
  #ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot .evcc-media {max-width:none!important;max-height:none!important;object-fit:contain!important;object-position:center!important;}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v45-text-enhance-repair-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const norm=v=>{const s=String(v||'').trim();return /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(s)?s.toUpperCase():'';};
  const composerSelector='#ethvelleTextComposer #evtcColorStrip .evtc-color-swatch[data-color]';
  const dockSelector='#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-swatch[data-color]';
  let currentComposerColor='#FFFFFF',currentDockColor='';
  let restoreComposerFocus=null;
  function setOnlyActive(root,selector,color){
    if(!root)return;const c=norm(color);if(!c)return;
    root.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(button=>button.classList.toggle('active',norm(button.dataset.color)===c));
  }
  function syncColorRings(){
    const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer');
    if(composer&&composer.classList.contains('active'))setOnlyActive(composer,'.evtc-color-swatch[data-color]',currentComposerColor);
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(dock&&currentDockColor)setOnlyActive(dock,'.evpa-swatch[data-color]',currentDockColor);
  }
  function settleRings(color,scope){
    const c=norm(color);if(!c)return;
    if(scope==='composer')currentComposerColor=c;else if(scope==='dock')currentDockColor=c;
    syncColorRings();
    requestAnimationFrame(syncColorRings);
    setTimeout(syncColorRings,40);
  }

  // Wrap the existing setters (do not replace their model updates / redraw / save behaviour).
  const priorComposerSet=window.setEthvelleComposerColor;
  if(typeof priorComposerSet==='function'&&!priorComposerSet.__evV45Stable){
    const wrapped=function(color){const c=norm(color)||'#FFFFFF';const result=priorComposerSet.apply(this,arguments);try{const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer');const t=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;if(composer&&composer.classList.contains('active')&&t){t.textColor=c;if(Number(t.textOpacity)===0)t.textOpacity=100;}}catch(_e){}settleRings(c,'composer');return result;};
    wrapped.__evV45Stable=true;window.setEthvelleComposerColor=wrapped;
  }
  const priorDockSet=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
  if(typeof priorDockSet==='function'&&!priorDockSet.__evV45Stable){
    const wrapped=function(key,color){const c=norm(color)||'#FFFFFF';const result=priorDockSet.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='textColor')settleRings(c,'dock');return result;};
    wrapped.__evV45Stable=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=wrapped;
  }

  // The earlier focus guard prevented Android from focusing the palette button, but that could
  // also suppress its synthetic click. Apply the selected colour on pointerdown itself, then
  // preserve the live draft input's focus so there is no keyboard close/reopen cycle.
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const target=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest(composerSelector+', '+dockSelector):null;
    if(!target)return;
    const isComposerSwatch=!!target.matches(composerSelector),color=norm(target.dataset.color);if(!color)return;
    const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer'),input=$('evtcStageText');
    const keepKeyboard=!!(composer&&composer.classList.contains('active')&&input&&document.activeElement===input);
    if(keepKeyboard){restoreComposerFocus=input;try{input.__evV45SelectionStart=input.selectionStart;input.__evV45SelectionEnd=input.selectionEnd;}catch(_e){}}
    // Apply now as a user gesture. The normal click may also fire; applying the same colour twice
    // is harmless, while this guarantees the colour is not lost if a WebView cancels that click.
    try{
      if(isComposerSwatch&&typeof window.setEthvelleComposerColor==='function')window.setEthvelleComposerColor(color);
      else if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColor==='function'){
        /* Stroke / Shadow / Background tabs own their colour: never write the tapped swatch to the text colour. */
        const tab=$('evpaTabs')?.querySelector('.evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'';
        const key=tab==='stroke'?'strokeColor':tab==='shadow'?'shadowColor':(tab==='highlight'||tab==='background')?'textBackgroundColor':'textColor';
        window.ethvellePicsartSetColor(key,color);
      }
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle text swatch:',err);}
    settleRings(color,isComposerSwatch?'composer':'dock');
    if(keepKeyboard){e.preventDefault();}
  },{capture:true,passive:false});

  function restoreFocus(){
    const input=restoreComposerFocus;restoreComposerFocus=null;
    if(!input)return;
    const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer');if(!input.isConnected||!composer||!composer.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const start=input.__evV45SelectionStart,end=input.__evV45SelectionEnd;
    try{if(document.activeElement!==input)input.focus({preventScroll:true});}catch(_e){try{input.focus();}catch(__e){}}
    try{if(Number.isInteger(start)&&Number.isInteger(end))input.setSelectionRange(start,end);}catch(_e){}
    syncColorRings();
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>{setTimeout(restoreFocus,0);},true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{setTimeout(restoreFocus,0);},true);

  // Keep rings correct after palettes are recreated without letting an old white default overwrite
  // the colour just selected in the active composer.
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    const swatch=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest(composerSelector+', '+dockSelector):null;if(!swatch)return;
    const color=norm(swatch.dataset.color);if(color)settleRings(color,swatch.matches(composerSelector)?'composer':'dock');
  },true);
  const enhancePanel=$('toolPanel');
  if(enhancePanel&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    new MutationObserver(()=>{
      const enhancing=enhancePanel.classList.contains('evlt-enhance-compact')&&getComputedStyle(enhancePanel).display!=='none';
      document.body.classList.toggle('evlt-enhance-preview-active',enhancing);
    }).observe(enhancePanel,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','style']});
  }

  // Also toggle the preview mode synchronously at the panel API boundary, so the selection frame
  // disappears before the enhanced compare overlay is shown and returns when Enhance closes.
  const priorOpenPanel=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof priorOpenPanel==='function'&&!priorOpenPanel.__evV45EnhanceChrome){
    const wrapped=function(key){
      document.body.classList.toggle('evlt-enhance-preview-active',key==='evltEnhance');
      const result=priorOpenPanel.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key!=='evltEnhance')document.body.classList.remove('evlt-enhance-preview-active');
      else requestAnimationFrame(()=>document.body.classList.add('evlt-enhance-preview-active'));
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV45EnhanceChrome=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
  const priorClosePanel=window.closePanel;
  if(typeof priorClosePanel==='function'&&!priorClosePanel.__evV45EnhanceChrome){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=priorClosePanel.apply(this,arguments);document.body.classList.remove('evlt-enhance-preview-active');return result;};
    wrapped.__evV45EnhanceChrome=true;window.closePanel=wrapped;
  }
})();

<script id="ethvelle-v46-targeted-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const safe=(fn)=>{try{return fn();}catch(_e){return undefined;}};

  // Opening the custom picker from Add Text deliberately dismisses the Android keyboard.
  const openPicker=window.openEthvelleColorPicker;
  if(typeof openPicker==='function'&&!openPicker.__evV46BlurKeyboard){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer'),input=$('evtcStageText');
      if(composer&&composer.classList.contains('active')&&input){
        try{input.blur();}catch(_e){}
      }
      return openPicker.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evV46BlurKeyboard=true;window.openEthvelleColorPicker=wrapped;
  }

  function qualityFor(model){
    if(!model)return '';
    const raw=model.__evltPendingEnhancedQuality||model.__evltEnhanceChosenQuality||model.enhancedAssetQuality;
    const runtime=String(model.__evltRuntimeEnhancedQuality||'').toUpperCase();
    if(runtime.includes('ULTRA'))return 'ULTRA HD';
    if(runtime.includes('QHD')||runtime.includes('1440'))return 'QHD';
    if(runtime.includes('FULL HD')||runtime==='HD')return 'FULL HD';
    const q=Number(raw)||0;
    return q>=2160?'ULTRA HD':q>=1440?'QHD':q>=1080?'FULL HD':'';
  }
  function placeBadge(el,label){
    if(!el)return;
    let badge=el.querySelector(':scope > .evlt-quality-row-badge');
    if(!label){if(badge)badge.remove();return;}
    if(!badge){badge=document.createElement('span');badge.className='evlt-quality-row-badge';badge.setAttribute('aria-label','Enhanced output quality');el.appendChild(badge);}
    if(badge.textContent!==label)badge.textContent=label;
  }
  function refreshQualityBadges(){
    let overlayList=[],backgroundList=[];
    try{overlayList=Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:(Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[]);}catch(_e){}
    try{backgroundList=Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:(Array.isArray(window.backgrounds)?window.backgrounds:[]);}catch(_e){}
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]').forEach(el=>{
      const model=overlayList.find(o=>String(o.id)===String(el.dataset.overlayId));placeBadge(el,qualityFor(model));
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .track-clip[data-bg-id]').forEach(el=>{
      const model=backgroundList.find(o=>String(o.id)===String(el.dataset.bgId));placeBadge(el,qualityFor(model));
    });
  }
  window.__ethvelleV46RefreshQualityBadges=refreshQualityBadges;
  const timeline=$('timelineInner');
  if(timeline&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    let queued=false;new MutationObserver(()=>{if(queued)return;queued=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{queued=false;refreshQualityBadges();});})
      .observe(timeline,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',refreshQualityBadges,{once:true});
  else refreshQualityBadges();

  // After Apply, force the actual timeline/canvas renderer to bind the runtime enhanced source.
  const priorApply=window.ethvelleApplyVideoEnhance;
  if(typeof priorApply==='function'&&!priorApply.__evV46CanvasRefresh){
    const wrappedApply=async function(){
      const result=await priorApply.apply(this,arguments);
      [50,180,420].forEach(delay=>setTimeout(()=>{
        safe(()=>{if(typeof window.renderOverlayLayers==='function')window.renderOverlayLayers();});
        safe(()=>{if(typeof window.refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead==='function')window.refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead();});
        safe(()=>{if(typeof window.applyBackground==='function')window.applyBackground();});
        safe(()=>{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();});
        safe(()=>{if(typeof window.updateSelBox==='function')window.updateSelBox();});
        safe(refreshQualityBadges);
      },delay));
      return result;
    };
    wrappedApply.__evV46CanvasRefresh=true;window.ethvelleApplyVideoEnhance=wrappedApply;
  }
  const priorStart=window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance;
  if(typeof priorStart==='function'&&!priorStart.__evV46QualityRefresh){
    const wrappedStart=async function(){const result=await priorStart.apply(this,arguments);[80,260].forEach(delay=>setTimeout(refreshQualityBadges,delay));return result;};
    wrappedStart.__evV46QualityRefresh=true;window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance=wrappedStart;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v47-final-repair-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const normColor=value=>{
    let c=String(value||'').trim();
    if(/^#[0-9a-f]{3}$/i.test(c))c='#'+c.slice(1).split('').map(ch=>ch+ch).join('');
    return /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c)?c.toUpperCase():'';
  };
  const activeComposer=()=>!!$('ethvelleTextComposer')?.classList.contains('active');
  function setVisibleColorRings(color){
    const c=normColor(color);if(!c)return;
    const specs=[
      ['#ethvelleTextComposer #evtcColorStrip .evtc-color-swatch[data-color]', 'data-color'],
      ['#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-swatch[data-color]', 'data-color'],
      ['#ethvelleTextStylePanel .evts-swatch[data-color]', 'data-color']
    ];
    specs.forEach(([selector,attr])=>document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('active',normColor(el.getAttribute(attr))===c)));
  }

  /* The composer has two legacy style objects (draft + dock target). Sync both so a colour picked
     in the Font row is what the check-mark saves, not just what the preview temporarily paints. */
  const priorComposerSet=window.setEthvelleComposerColor;
  if(typeof priorComposerSet==='function'&&!priorComposerSet.__evV47ApplyColor){
    const wrapped=function(color){
      const c=normColor(color);const result=priorComposerSet.apply(this,arguments);
      if(c){
        try{const target=window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget?.();if(target){target.textColor=c;target.textGradient='';target.textTexture='';if(Number(target.textOpacity)===0)target.textOpacity=100;}}catch(_e){}
        const input=$('evtcStageText');if(input){input.style.setProperty('color',c,'important');input.style.setProperty('-webkit-text-fill-color',c,'important');input.style.setProperty('background-image','none','important');input.style.setProperty('background-clip','border-box','important');input.style.setProperty('-webkit-background-clip','border-box','important');}
        setVisibleColorRings(c);
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV47ApplyColor=true;window.setEthvelleComposerColor=wrapped;
  }

  /* Make existing-text swatches immediately update the model, canvas renderer, and persistent save.
     In composer mode explicitly update the actual draft too; this is the path that legacy dock
     handlers could miss when a color was chosen from the Font section. */
  const priorDockSet=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
  if(typeof priorDockSet==='function'&&!priorDockSet.__evV47ApplyColor){
    const wrapped=function(key,color){
      const c=normColor(color);const composing=activeComposer();
      const result=priorDockSet.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='textColor'&&c){
        if(composing&&typeof window.setEthvelleComposerColor==='function'){
          try{window.setEthvelleComposerColor(c);}catch(_e){}
        }else{
          try{
            const target=window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget?.();
            if(target&&target.type==='text'){
              target.textColor=c;target.textGradient='';target.textTexture='';
              if(Number(target.textOpacity)===0)target.textOpacity=100;
            }
            if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();
            if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
          }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle text color apply:',err);}
        }
        setVisibleColorRings(c);
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV47ApplyColor=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=wrapped;
  }

  /* Re-sync the selected swatch on composer open/rebuild, using the real style target as source. */
  const priorComposerOpen=window.openEthvelleTextComposer;
  if(typeof priorComposerOpen==='function'&&!priorComposerOpen.__evV47SyncRings){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=priorComposerOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
        try{
          const target=window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget?.();
          const c=normColor(target?.textColor||'#FFFFFF')||'#FFFFFF';
          /* Only restore the selected swatch marker here. Calling the colour setter on open
             clears textGradient/textTexture, destroying the selected layer's saved fill effect. */
          setVisibleColorRings(c);
          if(typeof window.__ethvelleSyncTextSwatches==='function')window.__ethvelleSyncTextSwatches('textColor',c);
        }catch(_e){}
      });
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV47SyncRings=true;window.openEthvelleTextComposer=wrapped;
  }

  // Successful video enhancement now starts the main timeline player once. The short-lived
  // pause guard remains for unsuccessful runs and Apply's media-rebinding refreshes.
  function freezeEnhanceTargetVideo(forceClockPause){
    if(forceClockPause){
      try{
        if(typeof window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime==='function')window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime();
        else if(typeof window.pausePlayback==='function')window.pausePlayback();
      }catch(_e){}
    }
    try{
      const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');
      const raw=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls[id]:null;
      const video=raw&&raw.tagName==='VIDEO'?raw:(raw&&raw.querySelector?raw.querySelector('video'):null);
      if(!video)return;
      video.autoplay=false;video.removeAttribute('autoplay');
      const pausedPlayGuard=()=>{
        if(!(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)){
          try{video.pause();}catch(_e){}
        }
      };
      try{video.pause();}catch(_e){}
      if(!video.__evV47EnhancePauseGuard){
        video.__evV47EnhancePauseGuard=pausedPlayGuard;
        video.addEventListener('play',pausedPlayGuard);
        setTimeout(()=>{
          try{video.removeEventListener('play',pausedPlayGuard);delete video.__evV47EnhancePauseGuard;}catch(_e){}
        },2200);
      }
    }catch(_e){}
  }
  let enhanceAutoPlayToken=0,enhanceSweepRaf=0;
  let enhanceAutoPreviewPending=false,enhanceAutoPreviewRunning=false,enhanceAutoPreviewRaf=0,enhanceAutoPreviewStartTimer=0,enhanceAutoPreviewClipId=null;
  window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;
  function enhanceSelectedClip(){
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');
    return typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===id):null;
  }
  function cancelEnhanceAutoPreview(reason,pauseIfRunning,renderAfter){
    const active=enhanceAutoPreviewPending||enhanceAutoPreviewRunning||window.__evEnhancePreviewMute===true;
    if(!active)return false;
    const wasRunning=enhanceAutoPreviewRunning;
    enhanceAutoPlayToken++;
    enhanceAutoPreviewPending=false;enhanceAutoPreviewRunning=false;enhanceAutoPreviewClipId=null;
    if(enhanceAutoPreviewStartTimer){clearTimeout(enhanceAutoPreviewStartTimer);enhanceAutoPreviewStartTimer=0;}
    if(enhanceAutoPreviewRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(enhanceAutoPreviewRaf);enhanceAutoPreviewRaf=0;}
    window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;
    if(wasRunning&&pauseIfRunning&&typeof window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime==='function'&&typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying){try{window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime();}catch(_e){}}
    else if(renderAfter!==false&&wasRunning){try{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();}catch(_e){}}
    return true;
  }
  window.__evCancelEnhanceAutoPreview=cancelEnhanceAutoPreview;
  /* Every timeline scrub entry point writes this flag, including the compact zoom-HUD scrubber. */
  try{
    const oldScrubDescriptor=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(window,'__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing');
    if(!oldScrubDescriptor||oldScrubDescriptor.configurable){
      let scrubState=!!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing;
      Object.defineProperty(window,'__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing',{configurable:true,enumerable:true,get(){return scrubState;},set(value){const next=!!value;if(next&&!scrubState)cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('scrub',false,true);scrubState=next;}});
    }
  }catch(_e){}
  window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('pagehide',true,false),{once:true});
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('visibility-hidden',true,false);});
  function cancelEnhanceSweep(){if(enhanceSweepRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(enhanceSweepRaf);enhanceSweepRaf=0;}}
  window.__evSweepCancel=cancelEnhanceSweep;
  /* After Enhance finishes the BEFORE/AFTER divider glides 50% -> 0% -> 100% -> 50% so the whole
     enhanced frame is seen on both sides. Touching the divider cancels it at once. */
  function sweepEnhanceCompare(totalSec){
    cancelEnhanceSweep();
    const set=window.ethvelleSetCompareSplit;if(typeof set!=='function')return;
    const period=Math.min(6,Math.max(3,totalSec)),total=Math.max(period,totalSec),t0=performance.now();
    const step=now=>{
      if(!document.getElementById('ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot')){enhanceSweepRaf=0;return;}
      const t=(now-t0)/1000;
      if(t>=total){set(50);enhanceSweepRaf=0;return;}
      // phase 0..1 inside the current period; sine keeps motion smooth at both ends.
      const phase=(t%period)/period;
      set(50-50*Math.sin(phase*2*Math.PI));
      enhanceSweepRaf=requestAnimationFrame(step);
    };
    enhanceSweepRaf=requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }
  function playEnhancedVideoOnce(lockedId){
    // The clip that was enhanced is locked in when Enhance starts, so a neighbouring clip that happens to sit
    // under the playhead can never become the selected / previewed one.
    const clip=typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(lockedId)):null;
    if(!clip||clip.type!=='video'){window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;return;}
    const token=++enhanceAutoPlayToken,keepId=clip.id;
    enhanceAutoPreviewPending=true;enhanceAutoPreviewRunning=false;enhanceAutoPreviewClipId=keepId;
    // startPlayback()/pause deselect or re-select by playhead position. Put the enhanced clip back directly
    // (same pattern as the audio preview) so playback keeps running and the Enhance panel keeps its target.
    const keepSelected=()=>{
      try{
        if(String(selectedClip)===String(keepId))return;
        selectedClip=keepId;selectedKfIdx=null;
        document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .overlay-clip.selected,#timelineInner .track-clip.selected').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('selected'));
        const el=[...document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]')].find(n=>String(n.getAttribute('data-overlay-id'))===String(keepId));if(el)el.classList.add('selected');
      }catch(_e){}
    };
    const start=Number(clip.trimStart)||0,end=Number(clip.trimEnd)||start;
    if(!(end>start)){enhanceAutoPreviewPending=false;enhanceAutoPreviewClipId=null;window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;return;}
    const stopAt=start+0.9*(end-start);
    enhanceAutoPreviewStartTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
      enhanceAutoPreviewStartTimer=0;
      if(token!==enhanceAutoPlayToken)return;
      enhanceAutoPreviewPending=false;
      try{
        if(String(selectedClip)!==String(keepId))keepSelected();
        if(typeof window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime==='function'&&typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime();
        if(typeof window.seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds==='function')window.seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds(start);
        if(typeof window.startPlayback!=='function'){window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;enhanceAutoPreviewClipId=null;return;}
        window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=true;
        enhanceAutoPreviewRunning=true;
        window.startPlayback();
        keepSelected();
        // startPlayback() can be gated ("Preparing playback…") and start a moment later, so wait for it before treating "not playing" as stopped.
        const startWaitFrom=performance.now();let sawPlaying=!!(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying);
        // Preview once, stopping at 90% of the clip without moving the playhead afterward.
        const finish=()=>{
          if(token!==enhanceAutoPlayToken)return;
          enhanceAutoPreviewRunning=false;
          try{window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime();}catch(_e){}
          window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;
          try{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
          keepSelected();[60,250,700].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(()=>{if(token===enhanceAutoPlayToken)keepSelected();},ms));
        };
        const watch=()=>{
          enhanceAutoPreviewRaf=0;
          if(token!==enhanceAutoPlayToken)return;
          if(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)sawPlaying=true;
          if(typeof isPlaying==='undefined'||!isPlaying){
            if(!sawPlaying&&performance.now()-startWaitFrom<12000){enhanceAutoPreviewRaf=requestAnimationFrame(watch);return;}
            cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('playback-stopped',false,true);return;
          }
          keepSelected();
          const now=Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
          if(now>=stopAt-0.04){finish();return;}
          enhanceAutoPreviewRaf=requestAnimationFrame(watch);
        };
        enhanceAutoPreviewRaf=requestAnimationFrame(watch);
      }catch(err){
        console.warn('Ethvelle: automatic one-time Enhance preview playback failed',err);
        cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('preview-error',true,true);
        window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;
      }
    },150);
  }
  const priorStartEnhance=window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance;
  if(typeof priorStartEnhance==='function'&&!priorStartEnhance.__evV47PauseAfterRun){
    const wrappedStart=async function(){
      cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('new-enhance',true,true);
      let readyForPlayback=false;
      const lockedId=typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?selectedClip:null;
      try{
        const result=await priorStartEnhance.apply(this,arguments);
        const clip=typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(lockedId)):null;
        const status=$('evltEnhanceStatus');
        readyForPlayback=!!(clip&&clip.type==='video'&&status&&/Ready to apply/i.test(status.textContent||''));
        return result;
      }finally{
        if(readyForPlayback)playEnhancedVideoOnce(lockedId);
        else freezeEnhanceTargetVideo(true);
      }
    };
    wrappedStart.__evV47PauseAfterRun=true;window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance=wrappedStart;
  }
  const priorEnhanceTogglePlayback=window.togglePlayback;
  if(typeof priorEnhanceTogglePlayback==='function'&&!priorEnhanceTogglePlayback.__evEnhancePreviewCancel){
    const wrappedToggle=function(){cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('transport-toggle',false,false);return priorEnhanceTogglePlayback.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrappedToggle.__evEnhancePreviewCancel=true;window.togglePlayback=wrappedToggle;
  }
  const priorEnhanceTogglePlay=window.togglePlay;
  if(typeof priorEnhanceTogglePlay==='function'&&!priorEnhanceTogglePlay.__evEnhancePreviewCancel){
    const wrappedTogglePlay=function(){cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('transport-toggle',false,false);return priorEnhanceTogglePlay.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrappedTogglePlay.__evEnhancePreviewCancel=true;window.togglePlay=wrappedTogglePlay;
  }
  const priorEnhanceSelectClip=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof priorEnhanceSelectClip==='function'&&!priorEnhanceSelectClip.__evEnhancePreviewCancel){
    const wrappedSelectClip=function(which){if((enhanceAutoPreviewPending||enhanceAutoPreviewRunning)&&String(which)!==String(enhanceAutoPreviewClipId))cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('clip-selection',false,false);return priorEnhanceSelectClip.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrappedSelectClip.__evEnhancePreviewCancel=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrappedSelectClip;
  }

  /* Successful Apply closes only after the runtime enhanced source has actually been installed.
     Errors keep the panel open so the user can read the failure and retry. */
  const priorApply=window.ethvelleApplyVideoEnhance;
  if(typeof priorApply==='function'&&!priorApply.__evV47CloseOnSuccess){
    const wrapped=async function(){
      const result=await priorApply.apply(this,arguments);
      const status=$('evltEnhanceStatus');
      if(status&&/\bApplied\b/.test(status.textContent||'')){
        document.body.classList.remove('evlt-enhance-preview-active');
        cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('apply-close',true,true);
        if(typeof window.closePanel==='function')window.closePanel();
        return result;
      }
      freezeEnhanceTargetVideo(true);
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV47CloseOnSuccess=true;window.ethvelleApplyVideoEnhance=wrapped;
  }

  /* Update the in-memory saved playhead as it moves, so pagehide/device-save catches the latest
     position even when no other edit happened just before refresh. */
  const priorMovePlayhead=window.movePlayhead;
  if(typeof priorMovePlayhead==='function'&&!priorMovePlayhead.__evV47SavePlayhead){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=priorMovePlayhead.apply(this,arguments);
      try{if(typeof currentProject!=='undefined'&&currentProject&&currentProject.savedState){
        const t=Number(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
        currentProject.savedState.playheadTimeSec=Math.max(0,t);
      }}catch(_e){}
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV47SavePlayhead=true;window.movePlayhead=wrapped;
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setVisibleColorRings('#FFFFFF'),{once:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v49-font-palette-touch-fix-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const selector='#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-swatches.evpa-v25-font-built';
  let drag=null,suppressUntil=0,suppressPaletteClick=false;
  function getScroller(target){
    if(!target||!target.closest)return null;
    const scroller=target.closest(selector),dock=document.getElementById('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    return scroller&&dock&&(dock.classList.contains('evpa-v25-font-mode')||dock.classList.contains('evpa-font-palette-mode'))?scroller:null;
  }
  document.addEventListener('touchstart',function(e){
    if(!e.touches||e.touches.length!==1)return;
    const scroller=getScroller(e.target);if(!scroller)return;const t=e.touches[0];
    drag={scroller,x:t.clientX,y:t.clientY,scroll:scroller.scrollLeft,moved:false,button:e.target.closest?.('button')||null};
  },{capture:true,passive:true});
  document.addEventListener('touchmove',function(e){
    if(!drag||!e.touches||e.touches.length!==1)return;
    const t=e.touches[0],dx=t.clientX-drag.x,dy=t.clientY-drag.y;
    if(!drag.moved&&Math.abs(dx)>5&&Math.abs(dx)>Math.abs(dy)*1.12)drag.moved=true;
    if(!drag.moved)return;
    const max=Math.max(0,drag.scroller.scrollWidth-drag.scroller.clientWidth);
    drag.scroller.scrollLeft=Math.max(0,Math.min(max,drag.scroll-dx));
    suppressUntil=Date.now()+500;suppressPaletteClick=true;if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();
  },{capture:true,passive:false});
  function finish(){if(drag&&drag.moved){suppressUntil=Date.now()+420;suppressPaletteClick=true;}drag=null;}
  document.addEventListener('touchend',finish,{capture:true,passive:true});
  document.addEventListener('touchcancel',finish,{capture:true,passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    if(Date.now()>suppressUntil||!suppressPaletteClick)return;
    if(e.target?.closest?.(selector)){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();suppressPaletteClick=false;}
  },true);
  function apply(){
    const dock=document.getElementById('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(!dock||!(dock.classList.contains('evpa-v25-font-mode')||dock.classList.contains('evpa-font-palette-mode')))return;
    const scroller=dock.querySelector('#evpaPalette .evpa-swatches.evpa-v25-font-built');if(!scroller)return;
    [['display','block'],['overflow-x','auto'],['overflow-y','hidden'],['touch-action','pan-x'],['-webkit-overflow-scrolling','touch']].forEach(([k,v])=>scroller.style.setProperty(k,v,'important'));
    scroller.querySelectorAll(':scope > .evpa-font-color-tools,:scope > .evpa-font-color-row').forEach(row=>{
      [['width','max-content'],['min-width','max-content'],['max-width','none'],['touch-action','pan-x']].forEach(([k,v])=>row.style.setProperty(k,v,'important'));
    });
  }
  let queued=false;function queue(){if(queued)return;queued=true;(window.requestAnimationFrame||function(fn){setTimeout(fn,16)})(function(){queued=false;try{apply();}catch(_e){}});}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',queue,{once:true});else queue();
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.target?.closest?.('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab'))queue();},true);
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'&&document.body)new MutationObserver(queue).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
})();
</script>

  #timelineInner .layer-row.char-add-row .char-add-track{flex:1 0 auto!important;min-width:0!important;width:auto!important;max-width:none!important;}
  /* The contact sheet now has aspect-matched cells and is scaled to the clip's exact width once,
     with no repeat-generated gray gaps. Keep letterboxing near-black in the live-frame fallback. */
  @media(max-width:360px){
    #exportModalBackdrop.evlt-export-fullscreen .evlt-export-info-card.evlt-v50-video-details{padding:12px!important;}
    #exportModalBackdrop.evlt-export-fullscreen .evlt-v50-slider-row{padding:13px 0 12px;}
    #exportModalBackdrop.evlt-export-fullscreen #exportConfirmBtn.evlt-v50-export-btn{padding:0 11px!important;gap:5px!important;}
  }
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v50-export-and-font-scroll-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const qualities=[480,720,1080,1440,2160];
  const fpsValues=[24,30,60];
  const baseMbps=q=>q>=2160?16:q>=1440?12:q>=1080?8:q>=720?5:2.5;
  const maxMbps=q=>q>=2160?50:q>=1440?30:q>=1080?20:q>=720?12:8;
  let initialized=false,bitrateTouched=false,lastQuality=0;
  let savedFontScroll=0,fontRestoreUntil=0,fontRestoreTimer=0;

  function currentQuality(){try{return Number(typeof exportQuality!=='undefined'?exportQuality:720)||720;}catch(_e){return 720;}}
  function currentDuration(){try{return Math.max(0.1,Number(typeof getExportContentDurationSec==='function'?getExportContentDurationSec():0.1)||0.1);}catch(_e){return 0.1;}}
  function currentPlanMax(){try{const p=typeof getMyPlanData==='function'?getMyPlanData():null;return Math.max(480,Number(p&&p.maxQuality)||720);}catch(_e){return 720;}}
  function highestAllowedIndex(){const max=currentPlanMax();let idx=0;qualities.forEach((q,i)=>{if(q<=max)idx=i;});return idx;}
  function outputSize(q){try{if(typeof qualityToHeight==='function')return qualityToHeight(q);}catch(_e){}return {w:q,h:Math.round(q*16/9)};}
  function fmtTime(n){n=Math.max(0,Math.round(Number(n)||0));return Math.floor(n/60)+':'+String(n%60).padStart(2,'0');}
  function estimatedCost(){try{return Math.max(0,Number(typeof calculateExportCreditCost==='function'?calculateExportCreditCost(currentQuality(),currentDuration()):0)||0);}catch(_e){return 0;}}
  function creditEnabled(){try{return typeof getCreditSettings==='function'?!!getCreditSettings().enabled:true;}catch(_e){return true;}}
  function balanceText(){try{return typeof currentUser!=='undefined'&&currentUser?Number(typeof myCreditsBalance!=='undefined'?myCreditsBalance:0).toLocaleString():' - ';}catch(_e){return ' - ';}}
  function setText(id,value){const el=$(id);if(el)el.textContent=String(value);}
  function setRangeProgress(el){if(!el)return;const min=Number(el.min)||0,max=Number(el.max)||1,val=Number(el.value)||0;const pct=max<=min?0:Math.max(0,Math.min(100,(val-min)/(max-min)*100));el.style.setProperty('--evlt-v50-progress',pct+'%');}
  function refreshButton(){
    const btn=$('exportConfirmBtn');if(!btn)return;
    if(!btn.classList.contains('evlt-v50-export-btn')){
      btn.classList.add('evlt-v50-export-btn');
      btn.innerHTML='<span class="evlt-v50-export-label">Export</span><span class="evlt-v50-balance" title="Export credits required / credits available"><span class="evlt-v50-cost" id="evltExportButtonCost"> - </span><span class="evlt-v50-slash">/</span><span id="evltExportButtonBalance"> - </span><span class="evlt-v50-credits-unit">credits</span></span>'; 
      btn.setAttribute('aria-label','Export video. Credit cost and available credit balance are shown on the right.');
    }
    setText('evltExportButtonCost',creditEnabled()?estimatedCost().toLocaleString():'0');
    setText('evltExportButtonBalance',balanceText());
  }
  function updateDetails(){
    const q=currentQuality(),fps=Math.max(24,Math.min(60,Number(window.exportFrameRate)||30));
    const dims=outputSize(q),duration=currentDuration();
    let bitrate=Number(window.exportBitrateMbps);
    if(!(bitrate>0)){const base=q>=2160?16000000:q>=1440?12000000:q>=1080?8000000:q>=720?5000000:2500000;bitrate=base*(window.exportBitrateMode==='high'?1.4:1)*(fps/30)/1000000;}
    const mb=bitrate*1000000*duration/8/1024/1024;
    setText('evltExportResolution',dims.w+' × '+dims.h);setText('evltExportDuration',fmtTime(duration));setText('evltExportFrameRateReadout',fps+' fps');
    setText('evltExportSize',mb>=1024?(mb/1024).toFixed(2)+' GB':Math.max(.1,mb).toFixed(1)+' MB');
    const available=typeof currentUser!=='undefined'&&currentUser?Number(typeof myCreditsBalance!=='undefined'?myCreditsBalance:0).toLocaleString()+' credits':'Sign in required';
    setText('evltExportAvailableCredits',available);
    setText('evltExportCreditsRequired',creditEnabled()?estimatedCost().toLocaleString()+' credits':'Credits disabled');
    refreshButton();
  }
  function syncSliderUi(){
    const q=currentQuality(),qualityRange=$('evltV50Quality'),fpsRange=$('evltV50Fps'),bitrateRange=$('evltV50Bitrate');
    const maxIndex=highestAllowedIndex();
    if(qualityRange){
      qualityRange.max='4';
      let idx=qualities.indexOf(q);if(idx<0)idx=qualities.indexOf(720);idx=Math.max(0,Math.min(maxIndex,idx));qualityRange.value=String(idx);setRangeProgress(qualityRange);
      setText('evltV50QualityOutput',qualities[idx]+'p');
      const marks=$('evltV50QualityMarks');if(marks)[...marks.children].forEach((mark,i)=>mark.classList.toggle('locked',i>maxIndex));
    }
    const fpsValue=Math.max(24,Math.min(60,Number(window.exportFrameRate)||30));
    if(fpsRange){let idx=fpsValues.indexOf(fpsValue);if(idx<0)idx=1;fpsRange.value=String(idx);setRangeProgress(fpsRange);setText('evltV50FpsOutput',fpsValues[idx]+' fps');}
    if(bitrateRange){
      const max=maxMbps(q);bitrateRange.min='1';bitrateRange.max=String(max);bitrateRange.step='1';
      let val=Number(window.exportBitrateMbps);if(!(val>0))val=baseMbps(q);val=Math.round(Math.max(1,Math.min(max,val)));window.exportBitrateMbps=val;bitrateRange.value=String(val);setRangeProgress(bitrateRange);
      setText('evltV50BitrateOutput',String(val)+' Mbps');
      const tickValues=[1,1+(max-1)/3,1+2*(max-1)/3,max].map(n=>Math.round(n));
      ['evltV50BitrateMin','evltV50BitrateQ1','evltV50BitrateQ3','evltV50BitrateMax'].forEach((id,i)=>{
        const tick=tickValues[i];setText(id,String(tick)+' Mbps');
      });
    }
    updateDetails();
  }
  function chooseQuality(index){
    const idx=Math.max(0,Math.min(highestAllowedIndex(),Math.round(Number(index)||0))),q=qualities[idx];
    const old=lastQuality||currentQuality();
    const option=document.querySelector('#exportQualityGrid .ratio-opt[data-quality="'+q+'"]');
    if(option&&typeof window.selectExportQuality==='function')window.selectExportQuality(option);
    else {try{exportQuality=q;}catch(_e){}}
    if(!bitrateTouched&&old!==q)window.exportBitrateMbps=baseMbps(q);
    lastQuality=q;syncSliderUi();
  }
  function buildSettings(){
    const settings=$('evltExportAdvancedSettings');if(!settings||settings.dataset.evltV50Built==='1')return;
    settings.dataset.evltV50Built='1';
    settings.innerHTML='<div class="evlt-export-info-card evlt-v50-video-details"><h3 class="evlt-v50-details-title">Video details</h3><div class="evlt-export-info-grid"><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Duration</span><strong id="evltExportDuration">0:00</strong></div><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Estimated file size</span><strong id="evltExportSize"> - </strong></div><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Available credits</span><strong id="evltExportAvailableCredits"> - </strong></div><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Export cost</span><strong class="evlt-info-accent" id="evltExportCreditsRequired"> - </strong></div></div></div>'+ 
      '<section class="evlt-v50-output-settings" aria-label="Output settings"><h3 class="evlt-v50-output-title">Output settings</h3>'+ 
      '<div class="evlt-v50-slider-row"><div class="evlt-v50-slider-head"><label for="evltV50Quality">Quality</label><output id="evltV50QualityOutput">720p</output></div><input class="evlt-v50-range" id="evltV50Quality" type="range" min="0" max="4" step="1" value="1" aria-label="Export quality"><div class="evlt-v50-slider-marks" id="evltV50QualityMarks"><span>480p</span><span>720p</span><span>1080p</span><span>1440p</span><span>4K</span></div></div>'+ 
      '<div class="evlt-v50-slider-row"><div class="evlt-v50-slider-head"><label for="evltV50Fps">Frame rate</label><output id="evltV50FpsOutput">30 fps</output></div><input class="evlt-v50-range" id="evltV50Fps" type="range" min="0" max="2" step="1" value="1" aria-label="Frame rate"><div class="evlt-v50-slider-marks"><span>24 fps</span><span>30 fps</span><span>60 fps</span></div></div>'+ 
      '<div class="evlt-v50-slider-row"><div class="evlt-v50-slider-head"><label for="evltV50Bitrate">Bit rate</label><output id="evltV50BitrateOutput">5 Mbps</output></div><input class="evlt-v50-range" id="evltV50Bitrate" type="range" min="1" max="12" step="1" value="5" aria-label="Video bit rate"><div class="evlt-v50-slider-marks evlt-v50-bitrate-marks"><span id="evltV50BitrateMin">1 Mbps</span><span id="evltV50BitrateQ1">4 Mbps</span><span id="evltV50BitrateQ3">8 Mbps</span><span id="evltV50BitrateMax">12 Mbps</span></div></div></section>';
    const body=settings.closest('.evlt-export-body');
    const first=body&&body.querySelector('.field-label');
    if(body&&first)body.insertBefore(settings,first);else if(body)body.insertBefore(settings,body.firstChild);
    const qRange=$('evltV50Quality');qRange?.addEventListener('input',()=>chooseQuality(qRange.value));
    const fpsRange=$('evltV50Fps');fpsRange?.addEventListener('input',()=>{const idx=Math.max(0,Math.min(2,Math.round(Number(fpsRange.value)||0)));window.exportFrameRate=fpsValues[idx];syncSliderUi();});
    const bitrateRange=$('evltV50Bitrate');bitrateRange?.addEventListener('input',()=>{bitrateTouched=true;const max=Math.max(1,Number(bitrateRange.max)||maxMbps(currentQuality()));const value=Math.round(Math.max(1,Math.min(max,Number(bitrateRange.value)||baseMbps(currentQuality()))));window.exportBitrateMbps=value;bitrateRange.value=String(value);setRangeProgress(bitrateRange);setText('evltV50BitrateOutput',String(value)+' Mbps');updateDetails();});
    if(!initialized){if(!(Number(window.exportBitrateMbps)>0))window.exportBitrateMbps=baseMbps(currentQuality());initialized=true;}
    lastQuality=currentQuality();syncSliderUi();
  }

  // Preserve horizontal position when the font-controls render pipeline replaces its gallery.
  function getFontGallery(){return document.querySelector('#ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-font-gallery');}
  function activeFontTab(){return !!document.querySelector('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaTabs .evpa-tab.active[data-tab="font"]');}
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target?.closest?.('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab[data-tab="font"]')){savedFontScroll=0;setTimeout(()=>{const g=getFontGallery();if(g)g.scrollLeft=0;},0);}},true);

  // Rebuild the export controls after the original page has injected its account/quality markup.
  function boot(){
    buildSettings();refreshButton();
    const backdrop=$('exportModalBackdrop');
    if(backdrop&&typeof MutationObserver==='function')new MutationObserver(()=>{if(backdrop.classList.contains('active')){buildSettings();syncSliderUi();}}).observe(backdrop,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
    const grid=$('exportQualityGrid');if(grid&&typeof MutationObserver==='function')new MutationObserver(()=>{if($('exportModalBackdrop')?.classList.contains('active'))syncSliderUi();}).observe(grid,{attributes:true,subtree:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
    const oldEstimate=window.renderExportCreditEstimate;
    if(typeof oldEstimate==='function'&&!oldEstimate.__evltV50Wrapped){const wrapped=function(){const out=oldEstimate.apply(this,arguments);syncSliderUi();return out;};wrapped.__evltV50Wrapped=true;window.renderExportCreditEstimate=wrapped;}
    const oldOpen=window.openExportSheet;
    if(typeof oldOpen==='function'&&!oldOpen.__evltV50Wrapped){const wrapped=function(){const out=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(()=>{buildSettings();syncSliderUi();},0);return out;};wrapped.__evltV50Wrapped=true;window.openExportSheet=wrapped;}
    document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target?.closest?.('#exportQualityGrid'))setTimeout(syncSliderUi,0);},true);
    document.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target?.matches?.('#evltV50Quality,#evltV50Fps,#evltV50Bitrate'))setTimeout(syncSliderUi,0);},true);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v51-font-swatch-left-reset">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  let trackedDock=null,wasFont=false;
  function getDock(){return document.getElementById('ethvellePicsartTextDock');}
  function resetFirstSwatch(){
    const dock=getDock();if(!dock||!dock.classList.contains('evpa-v25-font-mode'))return;
    const scroller=dock.querySelector('#evpaPalette .evpa-swatches.evpa-v25-font-built');
    if(scroller)scroller.scrollLeft=0;
  }
  function watchDock(){
    const dock=getDock();if(!dock||dock===trackedDock)return;
    trackedDock=dock;wasFont=dock.classList.contains('evpa-v25-font-mode');
    if(typeof MutationObserver==='function')new MutationObserver(()=>{
      const isFont=dock.classList.contains('evpa-v25-font-mode');
      if(isFont&&!wasFont)requestAnimationFrame(()=>{resetFirstSwatch();requestAnimationFrame(resetFirstSwatch);});
      wasFont=isFont;
    }).observe(dock,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }
  // The text dock is created lazily when the text editor first opens, so delegate tab taps
  // and watch the document for the dock instead of requiring it to exist at page load.
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaTabs .evpa-tab[data-tab="font"]')){
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>{resetFirstSwatch();requestAnimationFrame(resetFirstSwatch);});
    }
  },true);
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function')new MutationObserver(watchDock).observe(document.documentElement,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  watchDock();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v52-color-picker-keyboard-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  let keyboardReturnState=null;
  let closeWasUserRequested=false;

  function composerInput(){
    const root=$('ethvelleTextComposer'),input=$('evtcStageText');
    return root&&root.classList.contains('active')&&input?input:null;
  }
  function rememberComposerFocus(input){
    if(!input||keyboardReturnState)return;
    let start=null,end=null,direction='none';
    try{start=input.selectionStart;end=input.selectionEnd;direction=input.selectionDirection||'none';}catch(_e){}
    keyboardReturnState={input,wasReadOnly:!!input.readOnly,start,end,direction};
  }

  /* Pointerdown runs before Android moves focus from the text field to the custom swatch. */
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const trigger=e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest(
      '#ethvelleTextComposer .evtc-wheel-swatch,'+
      '#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-color-wheel,'+
      '#ethvelleTextStylePanel .evts-wheel-btn'
    );
    if(!trigger)return;
    const input=composerInput();
    if(input&&document.activeElement===input)rememberComposerFocus(input);
  },true);

  const originalOpen=window.openEthvelleColorPicker;
  if(typeof originalOpen==='function'&&!originalOpen.__evV52KeyboardLock){
    const wrappedOpen=function(){
      const input=composerInput();
      if(!keyboardReturnState&&input&&document.activeElement===input)rememberComposerFocus(input);
      const state=keyboardReturnState;
      if(state&&state.input&&state.input.isConnected){
        try{state.input.readOnly=true;state.input.blur();}catch(_e){}
        document.body.classList.add('evpa-picker-keyboard-locked');
      }
      const result=originalOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      const picker=$('ethvelleColorPicker');
      if(state&&picker&&picker.classList.contains('active')){
        /* Reassert blur after the picker is mounted; readonly prevents Android IME reopening. */
        [0,80,220].forEach(delay=>setTimeout(()=>{
          if(picker.classList.contains('active')&&state.input&&document.activeElement===state.input){
            try{state.input.blur();}catch(_e){}
          }
        },delay));
      }else if(state){
        if(state.input&&state.input.isConnected)state.input.readOnly=state.wasReadOnly;
        keyboardReturnState=null;document.body.classList.remove('evpa-picker-keyboard-locked');
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedOpen.__evV52KeyboardLock=true;
    window.openEthvelleColorPicker=wrappedOpen;
  }

  /* Mark explicit close actions before their inline onclick handlers call closeEthvelleColorPicker. */
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    const picker=$('ethvelleColorPicker');
    if(!picker||!picker.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const close=e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest(
      '#ethvelleColorPicker .evpa-picker-close,'+
      '#ethvelleColorPicker .evpa-picker-apply'
    );
    if(close||e.target===picker)closeWasUserRequested=true;
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
    const picker=$('ethvelleColorPicker');
    if(e.key==='Escape'&&picker&&picker.classList.contains('active'))closeWasUserRequested=true;
  },true);

  const originalClose=window.closeEthvelleColorPicker;
  if(typeof originalClose==='function'&&!originalClose.__evV52KeyboardRestore){
    const wrappedClose=function(){
      const state=keyboardReturnState;
      const shouldRestore=!!(state&&closeWasUserRequested&&!state.wasReadOnly);
      closeWasUserRequested=false;
      const result=originalClose.apply(this,arguments);
      if(state&&state.input&&state.input.isConnected){
        state.input.readOnly=state.wasReadOnly;
        document.body.classList.remove('evpa-picker-keyboard-locked');
        keyboardReturnState=null;
        if(shouldRestore&&composerInput()){
          try{
            state.input.focus({preventScroll:true});
            if(state.start!=null&&state.end!=null)state.input.setSelectionRange(state.start,state.end,state.direction);
          }catch(_e){}
        }
      }else{
        keyboardReturnState=null;
        document.body.classList.remove('evpa-picker-keyboard-locked');
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedClose.__evV52KeyboardRestore=true;
    window.closeEthvelleColorPicker=wrappedClose;
  }

  /* Final first-swatch reset after the tab's renderer and palette rebuild have both completed. */
  function resetFontStart(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(!dock||!(dock.classList.contains('evpa-v25-font-mode')||dock.classList.contains('evpa-font-palette-mode')))return;
    const scroller=dock.querySelector('#evpaPalette .evpa-swatches.evpa-v25-font-built');
    if(scroller)scroller.scrollLeft=0;
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaTabs .evpa-tab[data-tab="font"]')){
      [0,40,120,240].forEach(delay=>setTimeout(resetFontStart,delay));
    }
  },true);
})();
</script>

  }

  /* The canvas text selection frame outlines the full effect footprint with a white keyline
     and a dark exterior shadow so it remains visible over bright and dark art alike. */

    display:block!important;border:1.35px solid rgba(255,255,255,.99)!important;

    outline:none!important;filter:none!important;opacity:1!important;
  }

<script id="ethvelle-v54-requested-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(v)||0));

  /* Keep rebuilt colour swatches free of historical inline offsets and ensure their first
     pixel is never positioned outside the scrollport. */
  function repairFontPalette(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(!dock||dock.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer'))return;
    if(!(dock.classList.contains('evpa-font-palette-mode')||dock.classList.contains('evpa-v25-font-mode')))return;
    const scroller=dock.querySelector('#evpaPalette .evpa-swatches.evpa-v25-font-built');
    if(!scroller)return;
    scroller.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('flex-flow','column nowrap','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('align-items','flex-start','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('justify-content','flex-start','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('width','100%','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('padding','5px 10px 5px 8px','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('box-sizing','border-box','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('scroll-padding-left','8px','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('height','110px','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('min-height','110px','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('max-height','110px','important');
    scroller.querySelectorAll('.evpa-font-color-tools,.evpa-font-color-row').forEach(row=>{
      ['transform','left','right','top','bottom','margin-left','margin-right'].forEach(prop=>row.style.removeProperty(prop));
      row.style.setProperty('position','relative','important');
      row.style.setProperty('height','43px','important');row.style.setProperty('min-height','43px','important');row.style.setProperty('max-height','43px','important');
      row.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');row.style.setProperty('width','max-content','important');row.style.setProperty('min-width','max-content','important');
      row.style.setProperty('margin','0','important');row.style.setProperty('padding','4px 10px','important');row.style.setProperty('left','0','important');row.style.setProperty('transform','none','important');
    });
    scroller.querySelectorAll('.evpa-font-color-tools>button,.evpa-font-color-row>button,.evpa-font-color-tools>.evpa-swatch,.evpa-font-color-row>.evpa-swatch').forEach(btn=>{
      ['transform','left','right','top','bottom','margin-left','margin-right'].forEach(prop=>btn.style.removeProperty(prop));
      btn.style.setProperty('position','relative','important');btn.style.setProperty('flex','0 0 35px','important');
      btn.style.setProperty('width','35px','important');btn.style.setProperty('min-width','35px','important');
      btn.style.setProperty('height','35px','important');btn.style.setProperty('min-height','35px','important');
    });
  }

  /* The legacy v12 document handler used a 7px-wide thumb tolerance and could miss the
     actual Android thumb centre. v33 now handles the drag with a measured thumb + finger pad. */
  function repairCharacterBadgeIcons(){
    const icon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="6" r="3"/><path d="M12 9v6M12 12l-5 4M12 12l5 3M12 15l-4 6M12 15l4 6"/></svg>';
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .layer-row.char-row .layer-badge').forEach(badge=>{
      const type=badge.dataset.modelType||'humanoid';
      if(!badge.querySelector('svg[data-char-icon="'+type+'"]'))badge.innerHTML=(window.evCharBadgeIconSvg?window.evCharBadgeIconSvg(type):icon);
      badge.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    });
  }

  /* Expand the text selection geometry by all painted effects outside its layout box.
     Text background padding is already part of the content node's box; stroke and shadow
     paint are not, so add a symmetric safety margin for them. */
  const previousSelBoxTarget=window.selBoxTarget;
  if(typeof previousSelBoxTarget==='function'&&!previousSelBoxTarget.__evV54EffectBounds){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const target=previousSelBoxTarget.apply(this,arguments);
      if(!target||target.kind!=='overlay')return target;
      let list=[];try{list=Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:(typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:[]);}catch(_e){}
      const id=String(window.selectedClip??(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?selectedClip:''));
      const model=list.find(item=>item&&String(item.id)===id&&item.type==='text');
      if(!model)return target;
      const stroke=Math.max(0,Number(model.strokeWidth)||0);
      const shadowOn=!!model.shadowEnabled;
      const blur=shadowOn?Math.max(0,Number(model.shadowBlur)||0):0;
      const sx=shadowOn?Math.abs(Number(model.shadowX)||0):0;
      const sy=shadowOn?Math.abs(Number(model.shadowY)||0):0;
      const hi=model.textHighlightEnabled?Math.max(0,Number(model.textHighlightPadding)||0):0;
      const extraX=Math.ceil(stroke+blur*1.6+sx+hi+3);
      const extraY=Math.ceil(stroke+blur*1.6+sy+hi+3);
      target.w0=Math.max(34,Number(target.w0)||34)+2*extraX;
      target.h0=Math.max(30,Number(target.h0)||30)+2*extraY;
      return target;
    };
    wrapped.__evV54EffectBounds=true;window.selBoxTarget=wrapped;
  }

  /* A single close click handler now wins before legacy document and button handlers. It
     preserves the selected text layer and restores the regular bottom layer toolbar. */
  window.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    const close=e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-close-tab');
    if(!close)return;
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    try{if(typeof window.ethvelleCloseTextEditingV17==='function')window.ethvelleCloseTextEditingV17();}
    catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle text editor close:',err);}
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(dock)dock.style.display='none';
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');
    const selectedId=String(window.selectedClip??(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?selectedClip:''));
    let selectedText=false;try{const list=Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:(typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:[]);selectedText=list.some(o=>o&&o.type==='text'&&String(o.id)===selectedId);}catch(_e){}
    if(row&&selectedText)row.style.display='flex';
  },true);

  /* Ensure four bitrate stop marks remain four even after the export sheet rebuilds. */
  function fixBitrateMarks(){
    const marks=document.querySelector('#exportModalBackdrop .evlt-v50-bitrate-marks');
    if(!marks)return;
    const mid=marks.querySelector('#evltV50BitrateMid');if(mid)mid.remove();
    marks.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
    marks.querySelectorAll('span').forEach(span=>span.style.setProperty('flex','1 1 25%','important'));
  }

  let queued=false;
  function queue(){if(queued)return;queued=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{queued=false;repairFontPalette();repairCharacterBadgeIcons();fixBitrateMarks();});}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',queue,{once:true});else queue();
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'&&document.body){
    const observer=new MutationObserver(queue);observer.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(e.target?.closest?.('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaTabs .evpa-tab[data-tab="font"]')){
      [0,40,120].forEach(delay=>setTimeout(queue,delay));
    }
  },true);
  window.addEventListener('resize',queue,{passive:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v56-interaction-fixes">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const icon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="6" r="3"/><path d="M12 9v6M12 12l-5 4M12 12l5 3M12 15l-4 6M12 15l4 6"/></svg>';
  function ensureAddCharacterIcon(){
    const badge=document.querySelector('#timelineInner .char-add-row .layer-badge');
    if(badge&&!badge.querySelector('svg circle[cx="12"][cy="6"]'))badge.innerHTML=icon;
  }
  function fontPalette(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(!dock||dock.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer'))return;
    if(!(dock.classList.contains('evpa-font-palette-mode')||dock.classList.contains('evpa-v25-font-mode')))return;
    const scroller=dock.querySelector('#evpaPalette .evpa-swatches.evpa-v25-font-built');
    if(!scroller)return;
    scroller.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('flex-flow','column nowrap','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('align-items','flex-start','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('justify-content','flex-start','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('width','100%','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('box-sizing','border-box','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('padding','5px 10px 5px 8px','important');
    scroller.style.setProperty('scroll-padding-left','8px','important');
    scroller.querySelectorAll('.evpa-font-color-tools,.evpa-font-color-row').forEach(row=>{
      ['transform','left','right','top','bottom','margin-left','margin-right'].forEach(k=>row.style.removeProperty(k));
      row.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
      row.style.setProperty('position','relative','important');
      row.style.setProperty('left','0','important');
      row.style.setProperty('transform','none','important');
      row.style.setProperty('width','max-content','important');
      row.style.setProperty('min-width','max-content','important');
      row.style.setProperty('height','43px','important');
      row.style.setProperty('min-height','43px','important');
      row.style.setProperty('max-height','43px','important');
      row.style.setProperty('margin','0','important');
      row.style.setProperty('padding','4px 10px','important');
    });
  }
  function fixBitrateMarks(){
    const marks=document.querySelector('#exportModalBackdrop .evlt-v50-bitrate-marks');
    if(!marks)return;
    const oldMid=marks.querySelector('#evltV50BitrateMid');if(oldMid)oldMid.remove();
    marks.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
    marks.style.setProperty('justify-content','space-between','important');
    marks.querySelectorAll('span').forEach(span=>{
      span.style.setProperty('flex','1 1 25%','important');
      span.style.setProperty('min-width','0','important');
      span.style.setProperty('text-align','center','important');
      span.style.setProperty('white-space','nowrap','important');
      span.style.setProperty('font-variant-numeric','tabular-nums','important');
    });
  }
  function closeTextDock(e){
    if(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();}
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock'),row=$('bottomToolsClip');
    const selectedId=String(window.selectedClip??'');
    let selectedText=false;
    try{
      const list=Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[];
      selectedText=list.some(item=>item&&item.type==='text'&&String(item.id)===selectedId);
    }catch(_e){}
    try{
      if(typeof window.ethvelleCloseTextEditingV17==='function')window.ethvelleCloseTextEditingV17();
      else if(typeof window.ethvelleCloseTextEditingV14==='function')window.ethvelleCloseTextEditingV14();
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle text editor close:',err);}
    if(dock){dock.classList.remove('evpa-in-composer');dock.style.setProperty('display','none','important');}
    if(row&&selectedText)row.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      if(dock)dock.style.setProperty('display','none','important');
      if(row&&selectedText)row.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
    });
  }
  function ensureAccessibleClose(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(!dock||dock.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer'))return;
    const head=dock.querySelector('.evpa-sheet-head');if(!head)return;
    let close=head.querySelector('.evpa-v56-close');
    if(!close){
      close=document.createElement('button');close.type='button';close.className='evpa-v56-close';
      close.setAttribute('aria-label','Close text editing');close.title='Close editing';close.textContent='×';
      close.addEventListener('click',closeTextDock);
      head.appendChild(close);
    }
  }
  let queued=false;
  function queue(){if(queued)return;queued=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{queued=false;ensureAddCharacterIcon();fontPalette();fixBitrateMarks();ensureAccessibleClose();});}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',queue,{once:true});else queue();
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'&&document.body){
    const mo=new MutationObserver(queue);
    mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    const tab=e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaTabs .evpa-tab[data-tab="font"]');
    if(!tab)return;
    const wasActive=tab.classList.contains('active');
    if(!wasActive){
      [0,40,120,240].forEach(delay=>setTimeout(()=>{
        const scroller=$('evpaPalette')?.querySelector('.evpa-swatches.evpa-v25-font-built');
        if(scroller)scroller.scrollLeft=0;
        fontPalette();
      },delay));
    }else setTimeout(fontPalette,0);
  },true);
  window.addEventListener('resize',queue,{passive:true});
})();
</script>

<style id="ethvelle-v57-requested-ui-fixes">

  #ethvellePicsartTextDock.evpa-v25-font-mode:not(.evpa-in-composer) #evpaPalette .evpa-swatches.evpa-v25-font-built > .evpa-font-color-row,
